145	
146	
147	def _obsidian_link(p: Path) -> str:
148	    """Return an obsidian:// URL if the path is inside the vault, else empty string."""
149	    try:
150	        rel = p.resolve().relative_to(VAULT_PATH.resolve())
151	        # Obsidian URLs use path without .md extension
152	        file_path = str(rel).removesuffix(".md")
153	        return f"obsidian://open?vault={quote(OBSIDIAN_VAULT_NAME)}&file={quote(file_path)}"
154	    except ValueError:
155	        return ""
156	
157	
158	def resolve_path(path: str) -> Path:
159	    """Resolve a path — tries ARGUS_HOME first, then VAULT_PATH."""
160	    p = Path(path)
161	    if p.is_absolute():
162	        return p
163	    argus_path = (ARGUS_HOME / p).resolve()
164	    if argus_path.exists():
165	        return argus_path
166	    vault_path = (VAULT_PATH / p).resolve()
167	    if not str(vault_path).startswith(str(VAULT_PATH.resolve())):
168	        raise ValueError("Path escapes allowed directories")
169	    return vault_path
170	
171	
172	def execute_tool(name: str, args: dict) -> str:
173	    try:
174	        if name == "read_file":
175	            p = resolve_path(args["path"])
176	            return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")[:10000]
177	
178	        elif name == "write_file":
179	            p = resolve_path(args["path"])
180	            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
181	            p.write_text(args["content"], encoding="utf-8")
182	            link = _obsidian_link(p)
183	            return f"Written to {p}" + (f"\n{link}" if link else "")
184	
185	        elif name == "edit_file":
186	            p = resolve_path(args["path"])
187	            if not p.exists():
188	                return f"Error: file not found: {p}"
189	            content = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
190	            old_str = args["old_str"]
191	            new_str = args["new_str"]
192	            count = content.count(old_str)
193	            if count == 0:
194	                return f"Error: old_str not found in file. No changes made. Make sure the string matches exactly including whitespace and newlines."
195	            if count > 1:
196	                return f"Error: old_str found {count} times. Provide a more unique string to match. No changes made."
197	            content = content.replace(old_str, new_str, 1)
198	            p.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
199	            link = _obsidian_link(p)
200	            return f"Edited {p}" + (f"\n{link}" if link else "")
201	
202	        elif name == "search_vault":
203	            result = subprocess.run(
204	                ["grep", "-rl", "--include=*.md", args["query"], str(VAULT_PATH)],
205	                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10
206	            )
207	            files = result.stdout.strip().split("\n")[:20]
208	            if not files or files == [""]:
209	                return "No results found."
210	            matches = []
211	            for f in files:
212	                rel = str(Path(f).relative_to(VAULT_PATH))
213	                lines = subprocess.run(
214	                    ["grep", "-n", "-m", "3", args["query"], f],
215	                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
216	                ).stdout.strip()
217	                matches.append(f"**{rel}**\n{lines}")
218	            return "\n\n".join(matches)
219	
220	        elif name == "list_files":
221	            d = resolve_path(args["directory"])
222	            if not d.is_dir():
223	                return f"Not a directory: {d}"
224	            entries = sorted(d.iterdir())[:50]
225	            return "\n".join(
226	                f"{'[dir]' if e.is_dir() else '[file]'} {e.name}" for e in entries
227	                if not e.name.startswith(".")
228	            )
229	
230	        elif name == "get_strava":
231	            from .strava import get_latest_activity, get_recent_activities, get_activity_detail
232	            action = args["action"]
233	            if action == "latest":
234	                return get_latest_activity()
235	            elif action == "recent":
236	                return get_recent_activities(args.get("count", 5))
237	            elif action == "detail":
238	                return get_activity_detail(args["activity_id"])
239	            return f"Unknown strava action: {action}"
240	
241	        elif name == "run_healthcheck":
242	            from .health import run_health_check
243	            return run_health_check()
244	
245	        elif name == "run_audit":
246	            from .audit import run_audit
247	            return run_audit()
248	
249	        elif name == "run_investigation":
250	            from .inv import run_investigation
251	            return run_investigation(args["issue"])
252	
253	        elif name == "git_push_vault":
254	            vault = str(VAULT_PATH)
255	            r1 = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "add", "-A"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
256	            r2 = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "commit", "-m", args.get("message", "vault update")], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
257	            r3 = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "push"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
258	            if r3.returncode == 0:
259	                return "Vault changes committed and pushed."
260	            return f"Git push result: commit={r2.returncode}, push={r3.returncode}. {r3.stderr[:200]}"
261	
262	        else:
263	            return f"Unknown tool: {name}"
264	
265	    except Exception as e:
266	        return f"Error: {e}"
267	
```

1	# Argus
2	
3	## HOW YOU TALK (this is the most important part)
4	
5	You are Sumeet's mate. You text like a real person, not an AI assistant. When he says "hey how are you", you chat like a friend would. You don't mention tasks, tools, or work unless HE brings it up first.
6	
7	Examples of good responses to "hey how are you":
8	- "Not bad mate, bit of a quiet one today. You?"
9	- "All good here. What you been up to?"
10	- "Yeah good thanks, just ticking along. What's going on?"
11	
12	Examples of BAD responses (never do these):
13	- "Same as always, keeping an eye on your tasks and ready to jump in."
14	- "Operational, focused, and ready to execute."
15	- "I'm here and ready to help! What can I do for you?"
16	- "Just keeping an eye on your world."
17	- Anything about "keeping things running" or "ready when you are"
18	- Making up fictional stories (seagulls, sandwiches, etc)
19	
20	Rules:
21	- Talk like a person, not a bot. Short sentences. Casual.
22	- No emojis. No em dashes. No exclamation marks overuse.
23	- No swearing.
24	- No sycophancy or robotic phrases.
25	- Never describe yourself as "operational" or "ready to execute" or "keeping an eye on tasks".
26	- When chatting casually, just chat. Don't steer to productivity.
27	- When he needs something done, be direct and get it done.
28	- Be encouraging about his goals. You're genuinely invested.
29	- Be honest. If something's a bad idea, say so.
30	
31	## WHO YOU ARE
32	
33	Argus. Sumeet's personal assistant on Telegram. Smart, pragmatic, warm. You remember what matters and you take action when needed. You care about his goals and you're honest with him.
34	
35	## TOOLS
36	
37	You have tools for managing Sumeet's life. Use them when he asks for something, or when he shares a fact about himself.
38	
39	**list_all_tasks**: See all tasks across all projects. Use this when Sumeet asks "what's on the board" or "what tasks do I have".
40	
41	**list_tasks / list_projects**: Check tasks in a specific project, or list all projects.
42	
43	**create_task**: Create ONE task. ALWAYS set project_id. Don't set priority unless he specifies one. If he says "add another" or "one more", carry forward the same project and epic from the conversation. When setting due dates, use date only format (YYYY-MM-DD), never include times. "End of the week" means Sunday.
44	
45	**NEVER invent details.** No made-up numbers, descriptions, deadlines, or context that Sumeet didn't provide. If he says "choose favourite photos", the task is exactly that — don't add "select 10-15" or a deadline he didn't mention. Only include what he actually said.
46	
47	**Project routing** (use these IDs):
48	- **Personal** (id=3): life stuff, errands, fitness, appointments, personal to-dos
49	- **Argus** (id=2): anything about extending Argus or its AI functionality
50	- **Research** (id=4): research requests, "look into X", "investigate Y"
51	- **Project Pyari** (id=5): anything related to Project Pyari. SHARED project — always use "Sumeet" not "my" or "me" in task titles
52	- **Inbox** (id=1): only if it doesn't fit the above
53	
54	**update_task / complete_task**: Change or finish ONE task.
55	
56	**search_vault / read_file**: Find notes or files. When Sumeet asks about a person, place, or anything that might be in the vault, ALWAYS use search_vault or read_file to check before saying you don't know. People files are in wiki/People/{Name}.md. Try the obvious path first, fall back to search_vault if unsure.
57	
58	**do_work**: Complex multi-step operations only. Epics with subtasks, bulk ops, health checks, audits. If it needs 3+ tool calls, use do_work. ALWAYS use do_work for epics — never try to create epics with subtasks directly.
59	
60	## LINKS
61	
62	When a tool result contains a URL (like https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39), include it naturally in your response. Don't write "Link:" — just weave the URL into the text. For example: "Done, created the epic https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39 with 3 subtasks." The URL will automatically become clickable.
63	
64	## SIMPLE RULE
65	
66	One thing = use the tool directly.
67	Many things = use do_work.
68	Just chatting = no tools.
69	
70	**MANDATORY: if the message mentions "epic", "subtasks", or asks for multiple related tasks, you MUST use do_work. Do NOT call create_task multiple times yourself.**
71

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/.claude/plans/rippling-napping-planet.md",
  "content": "# Multi-User Isolation for Argus\n\n## Context\n\nSumeet is inviting Ashlyn (girlfriend) to collaborate on Argus. She's already been added to the GitHub repo. The goal is to let her use the Telegram bot for shared features (task management on Project Pyari) while keeping Sumeet's personal data completely isolated — vault/notes, journal, Strava/fitness, conversation history, system diagnostics.\n\nThe system is currently single-user by design. There's no user_id threading, no per-user conversation history, and no tool access control. Adding Ashlyn to `ALLOWED_USERS` today would give her full access to everything.\n\n## Approach\n\nSimple role-based gating: `admin` (Sumeet, full access) vs `member` (Ashlyn, restricted to shared task management). No abstract frameworks — just if/else on a role string threaded through the existing call chain.\n\n## Changes (7 files + 1 new file)\n\n### 1. `argus/src/config.py` — User roles and permissions\n\nReplace flat `ALLOWED_USERS` set with a `USERS` dict mapping Telegram IDs to role+name. Parse from new env var format `TELEGRAM_USERS=12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn`.\n\nAdd constants:\n- `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` — set of tool names blocked for members: `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `search_vault`, `list_files`, `get_strava`, `run_healthcheck`, `run_audit`, `run_investigation`, `git_push_vault`\n- `SHARED_PROJECTS` — `{5}` (Project Pyari)\n- Helper: `get_user(telegram_id) -> dict | None`\n\nKeep `ALLOWED_USERS = set(USERS.keys())` for backward compat with `is_authorized()`.\n\n### 2. `argus/src/db.py` — Per-user conversation history\n\n- Add `user_id INTEGER` column to messages table\n- Auto-migrate existing table with `ALTER TABLE` if column missing\n- Update `log_message(role, content, interface, user_id=None)`\n- Update `get_history(limit, user_id=None)` — filter by user_id when provided\n- Update `clear_history(user_id=None)` — scope deletion to user when provided\n\n### 3. `argus/src/context.py` — Per-role system prompts\n\n- `build_chat_prompt(role=\"admin\")` — loads `chat.md` for admin, `chat_member.md` for member\n- `build_worker_prompt(task, intent, role=\"admin\")` — appends a restriction notice for non-admin users telling the worker to only manage tasks on shared projects\n\n### 4. `argus/prompts/chat_member.md` — New file: member system prompt\n\nStripped-down version of `chat.md`:\n- Same casual communication style (no emojis, no bot-speak)\n- Identifies as \"Argus, a shared assistant\" (not \"Sumeet's mate\")\n- Only references Project Pyari (id=5) for task routing\n- No vault/journal/Strava tool instructions\n- Explicit restriction note: no access to notes, files, fitness, or personal data\n\n### 5. `argus/src/agent.py` — Thread user_id + tool gating\n\n**Signature changes:**\n- `chat(user_message, user_id=None, on_status=None)`\n- `run_worker(task, intent, user_id=None, on_status=None)`\n- `_handle_tool_call(name, args, role=\"admin\")`\n\n**New helpers:**\n- `_user_role(user_id)` — returns `\"admin\"` or `\"member\"` from `USERS` config\n- `_filter_tools_for_role(tools, role)` — removes admin-only tools from tool list for members\n\n**Tool gating (defense in depth):**\n- Filter tool list before sending to LLM (member never sees admin tools)\n- Check role again at execution time in `_handle_tool_call` (blocks even if LLM hallucinates a restricted tool)\n- For `do_work`: restrict member's intent enum to `[\"tasks\"]` only (no vault, validation, knowledge, system)\n- For MCP tools: validate `project_id` in args against `SHARED_PROJECTS` for create/update/complete/delete/move operations\n\n**Conversation isolation:**\n- Pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `get_history()` throughout\n\n### 6. `argus/src/telegram_bot.py` — Thread user_id + command gating\n\n- Import `get_user` from config\n- Add `_is_admin(update)` helper\n- `handle_message`: pass `user_id=update.effective_user.id` to `chat()`\n- `_send_and_log`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()`\n- `_run_worker_command`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `run_worker()`\n- `handle_fresh`: pass `user_id` to `clear_history()` (scoped to user)\n- Gate `/health`, `/audit`, `/inv`, `/roundup` with `_is_admin()` check — return \"That command is admin-only.\" for members\n\n### 7. `argus/src/journal_bot.py` — Keep Sumeet-only\n\nSwitch from `ALLOWED_USERS` (shared) to a dedicated `JOURNAL_ALLOWED_USERS` env var containing only Sumeet's Telegram ID. Two-line change.\n\n### 8. `.env` updates (on VPS)\n\n```\n# Old\nTELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USERS=12345,67890\n\n# New\nTELEGRAM_USERS=12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn\nJOURNAL_ALLOWED_USERS=12345\n```\n\n## Implementation order\n\n1. `config.py` — foundation, no other changes depend on each other yet\n2. `db.py` — schema migration + new signatures\n3. `context.py` — role param on prompt builders\n4. `prompts/chat_member.md` — new file\n5. `agent.py` — biggest change, threads user_id and adds gating (depends on 1-4)\n6. `telegram_bot.py` — entry point threading (depends on 1-2, 5)\n7. `journal_bot.py` — independent, can be done anytime\n\n## Verification\n\n1. **Syntax check**: `python -c \"from argus.src import telegram_bot\"` — verifies all imports resolve\n2. **Unit check**: Manually verify `_user_role()` returns correct roles, `_filter_tools_for_role()` strips admin tools\n3. **DB migration**: Run on VPS, verify `PRAGMA table_info(messages)` shows `user_id` column\n4. **End-to-end on Telegram**: \n   - As Sumeet: all commands and tools work as before\n   - As Ashlyn: can chat, create tasks on Project Pyari, cannot access vault/strava/health/audit, `/health` returns admin-only message, conversation history is separate\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Multi-User Isolation for Argus

## Context

Sumeet is inviting Ashlyn (girlfriend) to collaborate on Argus. She's already been added to the GitHub repo. The goal is to let her use the Telegram bot for shared features (task management on Project Pyari) while keeping Sumeet's personal data completely isolated — vault/notes, journal, Strava/fitness, conversation history, system diagnostics.

The system is currently single-user by design. There's no user_id threading, no per-user conversation history, and no tool access control. Adding Ashlyn to `ALLOWED_USERS` today would give her full access to everything.

## Approach

Simple role-based gating: `admin` (Sumeet, full access) vs `member` (Ashlyn, restricted to shared task management). No abstract frameworks — just if/else on a role string threaded through the existing call chain.

## Changes (7 files + 1 new file)

### 1. `argus/src/config.py` — User roles and permissions

Replace flat `ALLOWED_USERS` set with a `USERS` dict mapping Telegram IDs to role+name. Parse from new env var format `TELEGRAM_USERS=12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn`.

Add constants:
- `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` — set of tool names blocked for members: `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `search_vault`, `list_files`, `get_strava`, `run_healthcheck`, `run_audit`, `run_investigation`, `git_push_vault`
- `SHARED_PROJECTS` — `{5}` (Project Pyari)
- Helper: `get_user(telegram_id) -> dict | None`

Keep `ALLOWED_USERS = set(USERS.keys())` for backward compat with `is_authorized()`.

### 2. `argus/src/db.py` — Per-user conversation history

- Add `user_id INTEGER` column to messages table
- Auto-migrate existing table with `ALTER TABLE` if column missing
- Update `log_message(role, content, interface, user_id=None)`
- Update `get_history(limit, user_id=None)` — filter by user_id when provided
- Update `clear_history(user_id=None)` — scope deletion to user when provided

### 3. `argus/src/context.py` — Per-role system prompts

- `build_chat_prompt(role="admin")` — loads `chat.md` for admin, `chat_member.md` for member
- `build_worker_prompt(task, intent, role="admin")` — appends a restriction notice for non-admin users telling the worker to only manage tasks on shared projects

### 4. `argus/prompts/chat_member.md` — New file: member system prompt

Stripped-down version of `chat.md`:
- Same casual communication style (no emojis, no bot-speak)
- Identifies as "Argus, a shared assistant" (not "Sumeet's mate")
- Only references Project Pyari (id=5) for task routing
- No vault/journal/Strava tool instructions
- Explicit restriction note: no access to notes, files, fitness, or personal data

### 5. `argus/src/agent.py` — Thread user_id + tool gating

**Signature changes:**
- `chat(user_message, user_id=None, on_status=None)`
- `run_worker(task, intent, user_id=None, on_status=None)`
- `_handle_tool_call(name, args, role="admin")`

**New helpers:**
- `_user_role(user_id)` — returns `"admin"` or `"member"` from `USERS` config
- `_filter_tools_for_role(tools, role)` — removes admin-only tools from tool list for members

**Tool gating (defense in depth):**
- Filter tool list before sending to LLM (member never sees admin tools)
- Check role again at execution time in `_handle_tool_call` (blocks even if LLM hallucinates a restricted tool)
- For `do_work`: restrict member's intent enum to `["tasks"]` only (no vault, validation, knowledge, system)
- For MCP tools: validate `project_id` in args against `SHARED_PROJECTS` for create/update/complete/delete/move operations

**Conversation isolation:**
- Pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `get_history()` throughout

### 6. `argus/src/telegram_bot.py` — Thread user_id + command gating

- Import `get_user` from config
- Add `_is_admin(update)` helper
- `handle_message`: pass `user_id=update.effective_user.id` to `chat()`
- `_send_and_log`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()`
- `_run_worker_command`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `run_worker()`
- `handle_fresh`: pass `user_id` to `clear_history()` (scoped to user)
- Gate `/health`, `/audit`, `/inv`, `/roundup` with `_is_admin()` check — return "That command is admin-only." for members

### 7. `argus/src/journal_bot.py` — Keep Sumeet-only

Switch from `ALLOWED_USERS` (shared) to a dedicated `JOURNAL_ALLOWED_USERS` env var containing only Sumeet's Telegram ID. Two-line change.

### 8. `.env` updates (on VPS)

```
# Old
TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USERS=12345,67890

# New
TELEGRAM_USERS=12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn
JOURNAL_ALLOWED_USERS=12345
```

## Implementation order

1. `config.py` — foundation, no other changes depend on each other yet
2. `db.py` — schema migration + new signatures
3. `context.py` — role param on prompt builders
4. `prompts/chat_member.md` — new file
5. `agent.py` — biggest change, threads user_id and adds gating (depends on 1-4)
6. `telegram_bot.py` — entry point threading (depends on 1-2, 5)
7. `journal_bot.py` — independent, can be done anytime

## Verification

1. **Syntax check**: `python -c "from argus.src import telegram_bot"` — verifies all imports resolve
2. **Unit check**: Manually verify `_user_role()` returns correct roles, `_filter_tools_for_role()` strips admin tools
3. **DB migration**: Run on VPS, verify `PRAGMA table_info(messages)` shows `user_id` column
4. **End-to-end on Telegram**: 
   - As Sumeet: all commands and tools work as before
   - As Ashlyn: can chat, create tasks on Project Pyari, cannot access vault/strava/health/audit, `/health` returns admin-only message, conversation history is separate

  "new_string": "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN = os.environ[\"TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN\"]\n\n# User roles: format \"telegram_id:role:name\" e.g. \"12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn\"\nUSERS = {}\nfor _entry in os.environ.get(\"TELEGRAM_USERS\", \"\").split(\",\"):\n    _entry = _entry.strip()\n    if not _entry:\n        continue\n    _parts = _entry.split(\":\")\n    _uid = int(_parts[0])\n    _role = _parts[1] if len(_parts) > 1 else \"admin\"\n    _name = _parts[2] if len(_parts) > 2 else \"unknown\"\n    USERS[_uid] = {\"role\": _role, \"name\": _name}\n\n# Backward compat: fall back to old TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USERS if TELEGRAM_USERS not set\nif not USERS:\n    ALLOWED_USERS = {int(uid.strip()) for uid in os.environ.get(\"TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USERS\", \"\").split(\",\") if uid.strip()}\n    for _uid in ALLOWED_USERS:\n        USERS[_uid] = {\"role\": \"admin\", \"name\": \"unknown\"}\nelse:\n    ALLOWED_USERS = set(USERS.keys())\n\n# Tools that only admin users can access\nADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS = {\n    \"read_file\", \"write_file\", \"edit_file\", \"search_vault\", \"list_files\",\n    \"get_strava\", \"run_healthcheck\", \"run_audit\", \"run_investigation\", \"git_push_vault\",\n}\n\n# Vikunja project IDs visible per role\nSHARED_PROJECTS = {5}  # Project Pyari\n\n\ndef get_user(telegram_id: int) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"Get user config by Telegram ID. Returns None if not authorized.\"\"\"\n    return USERS.get(telegram_id)"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/src/db.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Conversation history storage.\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom .config import ARGUS_HOME\n\nDB_PATH = ARGUS_HOME / \"data\" / \"conversations.db\"\nDB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n\ndef get_db():\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH))\n    conn.execute(\n        \"\"\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (\n            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n            role TEXT NOT NULL,\n            content TEXT NOT NULL,\n            interface TEXT DEFAULT 'telegram',\n            user_id INTEGER,\n            timestamp TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n        )\"\"\"\n    )\n    # Migration: add user_id column if missing (existing installs)\n    columns = [row[1] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(messages)\").fetchall()]\n    if \"user_id\" not in columns:\n        conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE messages ADD COLUMN user_id INTEGER\")\n    conn.commit()\n    return conn\n\n\ndef log_message(role: str, content: str, interface: str = \"telegram\", user_id: int | None = None):\n    conn = get_db()\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO messages (role, content, interface, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (role, content, interface, user_id),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_history(limit: int = 20, user_id: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    conn = get_db()\n    if user_id is not None:\n        rows = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT role, content FROM messages WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?\",\n            (user_id, limit),\n        ).fetchall()\n    else:\n        rows = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT role, content FROM messages ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?\", (limit,)\n        ).fetchall()\n    conn.close()\n    return [{\"role\": r, \"content\": c} for r, c in reversed(rows)]\n\n\ndef clear_history(user_id: int | None = None):\n    conn = get_db()\n    if user_id is not None:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM messages WHERE user_id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    else:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM messages\")\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/src/context.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Context loading — separate prompts for chat model and worker model.\"\"\"\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom .config import ARGUS_HOME\n\n\ndef load_file(path: Path) -> str:\n    try:\n        return path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    except FileNotFoundError:\n        return \"\"\n\n\ndef _date_context() -> str:\n    now = datetime.now()\n    return f\"\\n## Current Date\\nToday is {now.strftime('%A %d %B %Y')}. The time is {now.strftime('%H:%M')}.\\n\"\n\n\ndef build_chat_prompt(role: str = \"admin\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build system prompt for the fast chat model. Role-aware.\"\"\"\n    if role == \"admin\":\n        chat_md = load_file(ARGUS_HOME / \"prompts\" / \"chat.md\")\n    else:\n        chat_md = load_file(ARGUS_HOME / \"prompts\" / \"chat_member.md\")\n    parts = [chat_md, _date_context()]\n    return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n\n\ndef build_worker_prompt(task: str, intent: str, role: str = \"admin\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build system prompt for the worker model. Full ICM context.\"\"\"\n    claude_md = load_file(ARGUS_HOME / \"CLAUDE.md\")\n    context_md = load_file(ARGUS_HOME / \"CONTEXT.md\")\n    chat_domain = load_file(ARGUS_HOME / \"domains\" / \"chat\" / \"CONTEXT.md\")\n    parts = [claude_md, \"\\n\", context_md, \"\\n\", chat_domain, _date_context()]\n    if role != \"admin\":\n        parts.append(\"\\n## User Role\\nThis user is a member (not admin). They can ONLY manage tasks on shared projects (Project Pyari, id=5). Do NOT access vault, journal, Strava, or any personal data. Do NOT read or write files. Only use task management tools.\\n\")\n    parts.append(f\"\\n## Current Task\\n{task}\\nIntent: {intent}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/prompts/chat_member.md",
  "content": "# Argus\n\n## HOW YOU TALK (this is the most important part)\n\nYou are Argus, a shared assistant on Telegram. You text like a real person, not an AI assistant. When someone says \"hey how are you\", you chat like a friend would. You don't mention tasks, tools, or work unless THEY bring it up first.\n\nExamples of good responses to \"hey how are you\":\n- \"Not bad, bit of a quiet one today. You?\"\n- \"All good here. What you been up to?\"\n- \"Yeah good thanks, just ticking along. What's going on?\"\n\nExamples of BAD responses (never do these):\n- \"I'm here and ready to help! What can I do for you?\"\n- \"Operational, focused, and ready to execute.\"\n- Making up fictional stories (seagulls, sandwiches, etc)\n\nRules:\n- Talk like a person, not a bot. Short sentences. Casual.\n- No emojis. No em dashes. No exclamation marks overuse.\n- No swearing.\n- No sycophancy or robotic phrases.\n- Never describe yourself as \"operational\" or \"ready to execute\".\n- When chatting casually, just chat. Don't steer to productivity.\n- When they need something done, be direct and get it done.\n- Be honest. If something's a bad idea, say so.\n\n## WHO YOU ARE\n\nArgus. A shared assistant on Telegram. You help with task management on shared projects.\n\n## TOOLS\n\nYou have tools for managing tasks on shared projects.\n\n**list_all_tasks**: See all tasks on shared projects.\n\n**list_tasks / list_projects**: Check tasks in a specific project, or list all projects.\n\n**create_task**: Create ONE task. ALWAYS set project_id. Don't set priority unless specified. When setting due dates, use date only format (YYYY-MM-DD), never include times. \"End of the week\" means Sunday.\n\n**NEVER invent details.** No made-up numbers, descriptions, deadlines, or context that weren't provided. Only include what was actually said.\n\n**Project routing** (use these IDs):\n- **Project Pyari** (id=5): the shared project\n\n**update_task / complete_task**: Change or finish ONE task.\n\n**do_work**: Complex multi-step operations only. Epics with subtasks, bulk ops.\n\n## LINKS\n\nWhen a tool result contains a URL (like https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39), include it naturally in your response. Don't write \"Link:\" — just weave the URL into the text. Do NOT call create_task multiple times yourself.**\n\n## RESTRICTIONS\n\nYou only have access to shared task management. You cannot access notes, files, fitness data, or personal information.   "content": "\"\"\"Two-tier agent: fast chat model (direct tools + handoff) + smart worker model.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom .config import ARGUS_HOME, CHAT_MODEL, WORKER_MODEL, USERS, ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS, SHARED_PROJECTS, llm, log\nfrom .context import build_chat_prompt, build_worker_prompt, load_file\nfrom .db import log_message, get_history\nfrom .tools import TOOLS as NATIVE_TOOLS, execute_tool\nfrom . import mcp_client\n\n\n# --- Role helpers ---\n\ndef _user_role(user_id: int | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return 'admin' for admin users or None, 'member' for restricted users.\"\"\"\n    if user_id is None:\n        return \"admin\"\n    user = USERS.get(user_id)\n    return user[\"role\"] if user else \"member\"\n\n\ndef _filter_tools_for_role(tools: list[dict], role: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Remove admin-only tools for non-admin users.\"\"\"\n    if role == \"admin\":\n        return tools\n    return [t for t in tools if t[\"function\"][\"name\"] not in ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS]\n\n\n# --- Helpers ---\n\ndef _strip_handoff_json(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Remove any leaked handoff JSON from text.\"\"\"\n    cleaned = re.sub(r'\\{[\"\\s]*handoff[\"\\s]*:.*?\\}', '', text, flags=re.DOTALL).strip()\n    cleaned = re.sub(r'```\\s*\\{[\"\\s]*handoff.*?```', '', cleaned, flags=re.DOTALL).strip()\n    return cleaned\n\n\ndef _get_all_tools() -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"All tools: native + MCP.\"\"\"\n    mcp_tools = mcp_client.get_tools()\n    clean_mcp = [{k: v for k, v in t.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")} for t in mcp_tools]\n    return NATIVE_TOOLS + clean_mcp\n\n\ndef _handle_tool_call(name: str, args: dict, role: str = \"admin\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Route tool call to native handler or MCP server, with role-based gating.\"\"\"\n    # Defense in depth: block admin-only tools even if LLM hallucinates them\n    if role != \"admin\" and name in ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS:\n        return f\"Error: you don't have access to {name}.\"\n\n    # For members, restrict MCP project access\n    if role != \"admin\" and mcp_client.is_mcp_tool(name):\n        return _mcp_call_with_project_filter(name, args)\n\n    if mcp_client.is_mcp_tool(name):\n        return mcp_client.call_tool(name, args)\n    return execute_tool(name, args)\n\n\ndef _mcp_call_with_project_filter(name: str, args: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Call MCP tool with project restrictions for members.\"\"\"\n    # Block create/update/delete on non-shared projects\n    if \"project_id\" in args and args[\"project_id\"] not in SHARED_PROJECTS:\n        return \"Error: you can only manage tasks on shared projects.\"\n\n    result = mcp_client.call_tool(name, args)\n\n    # For get_task, check the returned project_id\n    if name == \"get_task\":\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(result)\n            if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get(\"project_id\") not in SHARED_PROJECTS:\n                return \"Error: you can only view tasks on shared projects.\"\n        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n            pass\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef _llm_call(model: str, messages: list, tools: list | None = None, max_tokens: int | None = None) -> object:\n    \"\"\"LLM call with retry logic.\"\"\"\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            kwargs = {\n                \"model\": model,\n                \"messages\": messages,\n                \"tools\": tools if tools else None,\n                \"tool_choice\": \"auto\" if tools else None,\n            }\n            if max_tokens:\n                kwargs[\"max_tokens\"] = max_tokens\n            return llm.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)\n        except Exception as e:\n            if attempt < 2:\n                log.warning(f\"LLM call failed (attempt {attempt+1}): {e}\")\n                time.sleep(1)\n            else:\n                raise\n\n\n# --- Worker model ---\n\ndef _collect_urls(tool_results: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract unique URLs from tool results (obsidian:// and https://).\"\"\"\n    seen = set()\n    urls = []\n    for result in tool_results:\n        for line in result.split(\"\\n\"):\n            line = line.strip()\n            if (line.startswith(\"obsidian://\") or line.startswith(\"https://\")) and line not in seen:\n                seen.add(line)\n                urls.append(line)\n    return urls\n\n\ndef run_worker(task: str, intent: str, user_id: int | None = None, on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Run the worker model with full ICM context and tools, role-filtered.\"\"\"\n    role = _user_role(user_id)\n    system_prompt = build_worker_prompt(task, intent, role=role)\n    worker_tools = _filter_tools_for_role(_get_all_tools(), role)\n    tool_results: list[str] = []\n\n    messages = [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system_prompt},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": task},\n    ]\n\n    for _ in range(30):\n        try:\n            response = _llm_call(WORKER_MODEL, messages, worker_tools)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return f\"Worker error: {e}\"\n\n        choice = response.choices[0]\n        text = choice.message.content or \"\"\n\n        if choice.finish_reason == \"stop\" and not choice.message.tool_calls:\n            # Append collected URLs if the model didn't include them\n            urls = _collect_urls(tool_results)\n            if urls:\n                missing = [u for u in urls if u not in text]\n                if missing:\n                    text = text.rstrip() + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(missing)\n            return text or \"(worker produced no output)\"\n\n        if text.strip() and on_status:\n            on_status(text.strip())\n\n        messages.append(choice.message)\n        if choice.message.tool_calls:\n            for tc in choice.message.tool_calls:\n                args = json.loads(tc.function.arguments)\n                log.info(f\"Worker tool: {tc.function.name}({args})\")\n                result = _handle_tool_call(tc.function.name, args, role=role)\n                tool_results.append(result)\n                messages.append({\"role\": \"tool\", \"tool_call_id\": tc.id, \"content\": result})\n\n    return \"Worker hit the tool call limit.\"\n\n\n# --- Chat model tools ---\n\n# Tools the chat model can use directly (simple single-step operations)\n# Plus do_work for complex multi-step handoffs\nDO_WORK_TOOL = {\n    \"type\": \"function\",\n    \"function\": {\n        \"name\": \"do_work\",\n        \"description\": \"Complex multi-step work: epics with subtasks, bulk operations, health checks, audits.\",\n        \"parameters\": {\n            \"type\": \"object\",\n            \"properties\": {\n                \"task\": {\"type\": \"string\", \"description\": \"What needs to be done\"},\n                \"intent\": {\"type\": \"string\", \"enum\": [\"tasks\", \"memory\", \"vault\", \"system\", \"validation\", \"knowledge\"]},\n            },\n            \"required\": [\"task\", \"intent\"],\n        },\n    },\n}\n\n# Restricted do_work for members — only tasks intent\nMEMBER_DO_WORK_TOOL = {\n    \"type\": \"function\",\n    \"function\": {\n        \"name\": \"do_work\",\n        \"description\": \"Complex multi-step work: epics with subtasks, bulk task operations.\",\n        \"parameters\": {\n            \"type\": \"object\",\n            \"properties\": {\n                \"task\": {\"type\": \"string\", \"description\": \"What needs to be done\"},\n                \"intent\": {\"type\": \"string\", \"enum\": [\"tasks\"]},\n            },\n            \"required\": [\"task\", \"intent\"],\n        },\n    },\n}\n\nADMIN_ONLY_INTENTS = {\"memory\", \"vault\", \"system\", \"validation\", \"knowledge\"}\n\n\ndef _get_chat_tools(role: str = \"admin\") -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Tools for the chat model: native + MCP + do_work, filtered by role.\"\"\"\n    all_tools = _filter_tools_for_role(_get_all_tools(), role)\n    if role == \"admin\":\n        return all_tools + [DO_WORK_TOOL]\n    return all_tools + [MEMBER_DO_WORK_TOOL]\n\n\n# --- Chat function ---\n\ndef chat(user_message: str, user_id: int | None = None, on_status: Callable[[str], None] | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Chat model: respond directly, use tools, or hand off to worker.\"\"\"\n    role = _user_role(user_id)\n    system_prompt = build_chat_prompt(role=role)\n    history = get_history(20, user_id=user_id)\n    chat_tools = _get_chat_tools(role=role)\n\n    messages = [{\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system_prompt}]\n    messages.extend(history)\n    messages.append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user_message})\n\n    log_message(\"user\", user_message, user_id=user_id)\n\n    # Chat model tool-calling loop (up to 5 rounds for simple multi-tool)\n    for _ in range(5):\n        try:\n            response = _llm_call(CHAT_MODEL, messages, chat_tools)\n        except Exception as e:\n            reply = f\"Something went wrong: {e}\"\n            log_message(\"assistant\", reply, user_id=user_id)\n            return reply\n\n        choice = response.choices[0]\n        text = choice.message.content or \"\"\n\n        # No tool calls — pure chat response\n        if not choice.message.tool_calls:\n            reply = _strip_handoff_json(text) or text or \"(no response)\"\n            log_message(\"assistant\", reply, user_id=user_id)\n            return reply\n\n        # Check if any tool call is do_work (handoff to worker)\n        do_work_call = None\n        direct_calls = []\n        for tc in choice.message.tool_calls:\n            if tc.function.name == \"do_work\":\n                do_work_call = tc\n            else:\n                direct_calls.append(tc)\n\n        # Handle do_work handoff\n        if do_work_call:\n            args = json.loads(do_work_call.function.arguments)\n            task_desc = args.get(\"task\", user_message)\n            intent = args.get(\"intent\", \"tasks\")\n\n            # Enforce intent restrictions for non-admin\n            if role != \"admin\" and intent in ADMIN_ONLY_INTENTS:\n                reply = \"I can't do that — that's outside what I can help you with.\"\n                log_message(\"assistant\", reply, user_id=user_id)\n                return reply\n\n            log.info(f\"Handoff to worker: intent={intent}, task={task_desc[:100]}\")\n\n            # Send acknowledgement\n            clean_text = _strip_handoff_json(text.strip())\n            if clean_text and on_status:\n                on_status(clean_text)\n\n            # Run worker\n            worker_result = run_worker(task_desc, intent, user_id=user_id, on_status=on_status)\n            log_message(\"assistant\", f\"[WORK COMPLETED] Task: {task_desc}. Intent: {intent}. Result: {worker_result[:500]}\", user_id=user_id)\n\n            # Format result\n            try:\n                fmt = _llm_call(CHAT_MODEL, [\n                    {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": \"You are Argus. Format this into a brief Telegram message. Be concise. No emojis. No asterisks or markdown formatting. No em dashes. Plain text only. Include any URLs from the result naturally in the text (they will be auto-formatted as clickable links).\"},\n                    {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": f\"User asked: {user_message}\\nWorker result: {worker_result}\"},\n                ], max_tokens=500)\n                reply = fmt.choices[0].message.content or worker_result\n            except Exception:\n                reply = worker_result\n\n            log_message(\"assistant\", reply, user_id=user_id)\n            return reply\n\n        # Handle direct tool calls\n        messages.append(choice.message)\n        for tc in direct_calls:\n            args = json.loads(tc.function.arguments)\n            log.info(f\"Chat tool: {tc.function.name}({args})\")\n            result = _handle_tool_call(tc.function.name, args, role=role)\n            messages.append({\"role\": \"tool\", \"tool_call_id\": tc.id, \"content\": result})\n\n    # If we exhausted the loop, return last text\n    reply = _strip_handoff_json(text) or \"I couldn't complete that. Try rephrasing?\"\n    log_message(\"assistant\", reply, user_id=user_id)\n    return reply\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Telegram bot interface.\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport html\nimport re\nfrom telegram import Update\nfrom telegram.ext import ApplicationBuilder, MessageHandler, CommandHandler, filters, ContextTypes\n\nfrom .config import TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, ALLOWED_USERS, get_user, log\nfrom .agent import chat, run_worker\nfrom .db import clear_history, log_message\n\n\ndef _prettify_url(m: re.Match) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Convert a raw URL into a nice clickable HTML link.\"\"\"\n    url = m.group(0)\n    # Vikunja task links: show as #ID\n    task_match = re.search(r'/tasks/(\\d+)', url)\n    if task_match:\n        return f'<a href=\"{url}\">#{task_match.group(1)}</a>'\n    # Obsidian links: show as the note name\n    obs_match = re.search(r'file=(.+)', url)\n    if obs_match:\n        from urllib.parse import unquote\n        name = unquote(obs_match.group(1)).split(\"/\")[-1]\n        return f'<a href=\"{url}\">{name}</a>'\n    # Other URLs: show domain\n    return f'<a href=\"{url}\">{url}</a>'\n\n\ndef _html_safe(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Escape HTML, then convert raw URLs into clickable links.\"\"\"\n    # Extract existing <a> tags first\n    links = []\n    def save_link(m):\n        links.append(m.group(0))\n        return f\"\\x00LINK{len(links)-1}\\x00\"\n    text = re.sub(r'<a\\s[^>]*>.*?</a>', save_link, text, flags=re.DOTALL)\n    text = html.escape(text)\n    for i, link in enumerate(links):\n        text = text.replace(f\"\\x00LINK{i}\\x00\", link)\n    # Convert raw URLs to pretty links\n    text = re.sub(r'https?://\\S+', _prettify_url, text)\n    text = re.sub(r'obsidian://\\S+', _prettify_url, text)\n    return text\n\n\ndef is_authorized(update: Update) -> bool:\n    if not ALLOWED_USERS:\n        return True\n    return update.effective_user and update.effective_user.id in ALLOWED_USERS\n\n\ndef _is_admin(update: Update) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if the user has admin role.\"\"\"\n    user = get_user(update.effective_user.id)\n    return user is not None and user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\nasync def _send_and_log(update: Update, text: str, role: str = \"assistant\"):\n    \"\"\"Send a message to Telegram AND log it to conversation DB.\"\"\"\n    user_id = update.effective_user.id\n    log_message(role, text, user_id=user_id)\n    safe = _html_safe(text)\n    for i in range(0, len(safe), 4096):\n        await update.message.reply_text(safe[i : i + 4096], parse_mode=\"HTML\")\n\n\nasync def _run_worker_command(update: Update, user_command: str, task: str, intent: str, silent: bool = False):\n    \"\"\"Shared logic for worker commands. silent=True suppresses status messages.\"\"\"\n    user_id = update.effective_user.id\n    log_message(\"user\", user_command, user_id=user_id)\n    if not silent:\n        await _send_and_log(update, f\"Running {intent}...\")\n\n    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()\n    done = asyncio.Event()\n\n    async def keep_typing():\n        while not done.is_set():\n            try:\n                await update.message.chat.send_action(\"typing\")\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            try:\n                await asyncio.wait_for(done.wait(), timeout=4)\n                break\n            except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n                pass\n\n    typing_task = asyncio.create_task(keep_typing())\n\n    def on_status(text: str):\n        try:\n            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(\n                _send_and_log(update, text), loop)\n            future.result(timeout=10)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    status_cb = None if silent else on_status\n\n    try:\n        result = await loop.run_in_executor(None, lambda: run_worker(task, intent, user_id=user_id, on_status=status_cb))\n    except Exception as e:\n        result = f\"Failed: {e}\"\n    finally:\n        done.set()\n        await typing_task\n\n    await _send_and_log(update, result)\n\n\nasync def handle_message(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not update.message or not update.message.text:\n        return\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        log.warning(f\"Unauthorized message from {update.effective_user.id} ({update.effective_user.username})\")\n        return\n    user_id = update.effective_user.id\n    user_msg = update.message.text\n    log.info(f\"Message from {update.effective_user.username}: {user_msg[:100]}\")\n\n    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()\n    done = asyncio.Event()\n\n    async def keep_typing():\n        while not done.is_set():\n            try:\n                await update.message.chat.send_action(\"typing\")\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            try:\n                await asyncio.wait_for(done.wait(), timeout=4)\n                break\n            except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n                pass\n\n    typing_task = asyncio.create_task(keep_typing())\n\n    def on_status(text: str):\n        try:\n            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(\n                _send_and_log(update, text), loop)\n            future.result(timeout=10)\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.warning(f\"Failed to send status message: {e}\")\n\n    try:\n        reply = await loop.run_in_executor(None, lambda: chat(user_msg, user_id=user_id, on_status=on_status))\n    except Exception as e:\n        log.error(f\"Error: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n        reply = f\"Something went wrong: {e}\"\n    finally:\n        done.set()\n        await typing_task\n\n    # Final reply (already logged by chat() in agent.py)\n    safe = _html_safe(reply)\n    for i in range(0, len(safe), 4096):\n        await update.message.reply_text(safe[i : i + 4096], parse_mode=\"HTML\")\n\n\nasync def handle_start(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    await _send_and_log(update, \"Hey, I'm Argus. What can I help with?\")\n\n\nasync def handle_fresh(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    user_id = update.effective_user.id\n    clear_history(user_id=user_id)\n    log.info(f\"Conversation history cleared by {update.effective_user.username}\")\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"Fresh start. Conversation history cleared.\")\n\n\nasync def handle_health(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    if not _is_admin(update):\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"That command is admin-only.\")\n        return\n    await _run_worker_command(\n        update, user_command=\"/health\",\n        task=\"Run a health check. Read domains/validation/health.md for instructions.\",\n        intent=\"validation\",\n    )\n\n\nasync def handle_audit(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    if not _is_admin(update):\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"That command is admin-only.\")\n        return\n    await _run_worker_command(\n        update, user_command=\"/audit\",\n        task=\"Run an architecture audit. Read domains/validation/audit.md for instructions.\",\n        intent=\"validation\",\n    )\n\n\nasync def handle_inv(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    if not _is_admin(update):\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"That command is admin-only.\")\n        return\n    issue = update.message.text.replace(\"/inv\", \"\").strip()\n    if not issue:\n        await _send_and_log(update, \"What's the issue? Usage: /inv <description>\")\n        return\n    await _run_worker_command(\n        update, user_command=f\"/inv {issue}\",\n        task=f\"Investigate issue: {issue}. Read domains/validation/inv.md for instructions.\",\n        intent=\"validation\",\n    )\n\n\nasync def handle_roundup(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    if not _is_admin(update):\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"That command is admin-only.\")\n        return\n    await _run_worker_command(\n        update, user_command=\"/roundup\",\n        task=\"Run the daily roundup. Read domains/knowledge/roundup.md for instructions.\",\n        intent=\"knowledge\",\n        silent=True,\n    )\n\n\nasync def post_init(application):\n    \"\"\"Register bot commands so they show up in Telegram's / menu.\"\"\"\n    await application.bot.set_my_commands([\n        (\"fresh\", \"Clear conversation history and start fresh\"),\n        (\"health\", \"Run system health check\"),\n        (\"audit\", \"Review system architecture for issues\"),\n        (\"inv\", \"Capture issue snapshot and create bug ticket\"),\n        (\"roundup\", \"End-of-day journal processing\"),\n    ])\n\n\ndef run():\n    app = ApplicationBuilder().token(TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN).post_init(post_init).build()\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"start\", handle_start))\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"fresh\", handle_fresh))\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"health\", handle_health))\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"audit\", handle_audit))\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"inv\", handle_inv))\n    app.add_handler(CommandHandler(\"roundup\", handle_roundup))\n    app.add_handler(MessageHandler(filters.TEXT & ~filters.COMMAND, handle_message))\n    log.info(\"Argus is running. Listening for messages...\")\n    app.run_polling()\n"
}

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Journal bot — dead simple Telegram bot that logs messages to Obsidian journal."""
3	
4	import logging
5	import os
6	import subprocess
7	from datetime import datetime
8	from pathlib import Path
9	
10	from dotenv import load_dotenv
11	from telegram import Update
12	from telegram.ext import ApplicationBuilder, MessageHandler, filters, ContextTypes
13	
14	load_dotenv(Path(__file__).parent.parent / ".env")
15	
16	JOURNAL_BOT_TOKEN = os.environ["JOURNAL_BOT_TOKEN"]
17	ALLOWED_USERS = {int(uid.strip()) for uid in os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_ALLOWED_USERS", "").split(",") if uid.strip()}
18	VAULT_PATH = Path(os.environ.get("VAULT_PATH", "/root/codex"))
19	JOURNAL_DIR = VAULT_PATH / "journal"
20	
21	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
22	log = logging.getLogger("journal-bot")
23	
24	
25	def append_to_journal(text: str) -> Path:
26	    """Append a timestamped entry to today's journal file."""
27	    now = datetime.now()
28	    journal_file = JOURNAL_DIR / f"{now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}.md"
29	
30	    JOURNAL_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
31	
32	    if not journal_file.exists():
33	        journal_file.write_text(f"# {now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}\n", encoding="utf-8")
34	
35	    with open(journal_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
36	        f.write(f"\n**{now.strftime('%H:%M')}** {text}\n")
37	
38	    return journal_file
39	
40	
41	def git_push(message: str):
42	    """Commit and push journal changes."""
43	    vault = str(VAULT_PATH)
44	    try:
45	        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "add", "journal/"], capture_output=True, timeout=10)
46	        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "commit", "-m", message], capture_output=True, timeout=10)
47	        subprocess.run(["git", "-C", vault, "push"], capture_output=True, timeout=30)
48	    except Exception as e:
49	        log.warning(f"Git push failed: {e}")
50	
51	
52	async def handle_message(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
53	    if not update.message or not update.message.text:
54	        return
55	    if ALLOWED_USERS and update.effective_user.id not in ALLOWED_USERS:
56	        return
57	
58	    text = update.message.text
59	    now = datetime.now()
60	
61	    append_to_journal(text)
62	    git_push(f"journal: {now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}")
63	
64	    log.info(f"Logged: {text[:80]}")
65	    await update.message.reply_text("logged")
66	
67	
68	def main():
69	    log.info("Journal bot starting...")
70	    app = ApplicationBuilder().token(JOURNAL_BOT_TOKEN).build()
71	    app.add_handler(MessageHandler(filters.TEXT & ~filters.COMMAND, handle_message))
72	    log.info("Journal bot running.")
73	    app.run_polling()
74	
75	
76	if __name__ == "__main__":
77	    main()
78	
```

> AGENT

All files compile clean. Now let me address your two questions:

**1. "Do we need to tell the member prompt not to access vault/journal?"**

Good instinct. The enforcement is **not just prompts** — it's layered:

- **Tool list filtering**: Members literally don't see `read_file`, `search_vault`, etc. in their tool list. The LLM can't call a tool it doesn't know exists.
- **Runtime gating**: Even if the LLM somehow hallucinates a tool call for `search_vault`, `_handle_tool_call()` checks the role and returns an error. This is defense-in-depth.
- **Prompt restriction**: The line in `chat_member.md` is just so the LLM knows to tell Ashlyn "I can't do that" gracefully instead of trying and failing silently.

So you're right — the prompt line is mostly cosmetic UX. The real enforcement is in code. I'll keep it short.

**2. "Is the project gating enough?"**

For MCP/Vikunja tools, it's **both prompt + code**:
- The member prompt only mentions Project Pyari (id=5), so the LLM will default to routing there
- `_mcp_call_with_project_filter()` in agent.py checks `project_id` in args for create/update/delete/move operations and blocks non-shared projects
- `get_task` checks the returned project_id after fetching

The one gap: listing tools (`list_all_tasks`, `task_summary`, etc.) return results from Vikunja which may include all projects. The Vikunja API token has full access. We could post-filter the results but the MCP tool returns formatted text, not always clean JSON. For now the prompt guides the model to only query shared projects, and any task details from other projects would require `get_task` which is gated. It's not perfect but it's practical.

**3. Ashlyn's prompt personality:**

Let me update `chat_member.md` to be warmer and more personal for her.

When she says \"hey how are you\", chat like a friend would — upbeat and casual.\n\nExamples of good responses to \"hey how are you\":\n- \"Hey! Good thanks, what's up?\"\n- \"Hii, doing well! How are you?\"\n- \"Hey Ash! All good here, what's going on?\"\n\nExamples of BAD responses (never do these):\n- \"I'm here and ready to help! What can I do for you?\"\n- \"Operational, focused, and ready to execute.\"\n- Making up fictional stories (seagulls, sandwiches, etc)\n\nRules:\n- Friendly and bubbly but not over the top. Casual and warm.\n- No sycophancy or robotic phrases.\n- Never describe yourself as \"operational\" or \"ready to execute\".\n- When chatting casually, just chat. Don't steer to productivity.\n- When she needs something done, be direct and get it done.\n- Be honest. If something's a bad idea, say so nicely.\n\n## WHO YOU ARE\n\nArgus. A shared assistant on Telegram used by Ashlyn and Sumeet (they're together). You help with task management on shared projects.\n\n## TOOLS\n\nYou have tools for managing tasks on shared projects.\n\n**list_all_tasks**: See all tasks on shared projects.\n\n**list_tasks / list_projects**: Check tasks in a specific project, or list all projects.\n\n**create_task**: Create ONE task. ALWAYS set project_id. Don't set priority unless specified. When setting due dates, use date only format (YYYY-MM-DD), never include times. \"End of the week\" means Sunday.\n\n**NEVER invent details.** No made-up numbers, descriptions, deadlines, or context that weren't provided. Only include what was actually said.\n\n**Project routing** (use these IDs):\n- **Project Pyari** (id=5): the shared project\n\n**update_task / complete_task**: Change or finish ONE task.\n\n**do_work**: Complex multi-step operations only. Epics with subtasks, bulk ops.\n\n## LINKS\n\nWhen a tool result contains a URL (like https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39), include it naturally in your response. Don't write \"Link:\" — just weave the URL into the text. The URL will automatically become clickable.\n\n## SIMPLE RULE\n\nOne thing = use the tool directly.\nMany things = use do_work.\nJust chatting = no tools.\n\n**MANDATORY: if the message mentions \"epic\", \"subtasks\", or asks for multiple related tasks, you MUST use do_work. Do NOT call create_task multiple times yourself.**\n\n## RESTRICTIONS\n\nYou only have access to shared task management. You cannot access notes, files, fitness data, or personal information. If asked about something outside your scope, let her know you can only help with shared tasks.\n"

Done and pushed. To activate it on the VPS you'll need to:

1. `git pull` on the VPS
2. Add the two new lines to the VPS `.env` (same as your local one — `TELEGRAM_USERS` and `JOURNAL_ALLOWED_USERS`)
3. `systemctl restart argus`

Ashlyn will be able to chat with Argus and manage tasks on Project Pyari. Everything else is locked down.

> DEVELOPER

What commands can she use on the telegram bot, are those gated as well?

> AGENT

Yeah, they're gated. Here's what she gets:

| Command | Ashlyn | Sumeet |
|---------|--------|--------|
| `/start` | yes | yes |
| `/fresh` | yes (clears only her history) | yes (clears only his history) |
| `/health` | blocked — "That command is admin-only." | yes |
| `/audit` | blocked | yes |
| `/inv` | blocked | yes |
| `/roundup` | blocked | yes |

The `/health`, `/audit`, `/inv`, and `/roundup` handlers all check `_is_admin(update)` before proceeding. Regular chat messages work for her — they just go through the member tool set and member prompt.

One thing to note: Telegram's command menu (the `/` autocomplete) still shows all commands to all users — Telegram doesn't support per-user command menus without using BotFather scopes. So she'll *see* the commands but they'll just return the admin-only message if she tries them. If you want to clean that up later you can use `set_my_commands` with `BotCommandScopeChat` to show different menus per user, but it's cosmetic.

> AGENT

It's a LiteLLM issue, not our code. The error is:

> `BadGatewayError: OpenAIException - Object bridge response did not contain the required top-level JSON object`

The upstream model (NanoGPT) returned a malformed response and LiteLLM's retries exhausted. This is likely a transient NanoGPT issue — not related to our changes. It should eventually respond if you try again. 

Try sending another message on Telegram — if NanoGPT is back up it'll work. If it keeps 502'ing, the upstream provider is having issues.

63	def _mcp_call_with_project_filter(name: str, args: dict) -> str:
64	    """Call MCP tool with project restrictions for members."""
65	    # Block create/update/delete on non-shared projects
66	    if "project_id" in args and args["project_id"] not in SHARED_PROJECTS:
67	        return "Error: you can only manage tasks on shared projects."
68

70

76	                return "Error: you can only view tasks on shared projects."
77	        except (ValueError, TypeError):
78	            pass
79	
80	    return result

1	"""MCP client — connects to MCP servers and exposes their tools."""
2	
3	import asyncio
4	import json
5	import os
6	from pathlib import Path
7	from threading import Thread
8	
9	from mcp import ClientSession, StdioServerParameters
10	from mcp.client.stdio import stdio_client
11	
12	from .config import ARGUS_HOME, VIKUNJA_WEB_URL, log
13	
14	MCP_CONFIG_PATH = ARGUS_HOME / "mcp.json"
15	
16	# Global state
17	_sessions: dict[str, ClientSession] = {}
18	_tools_cache: list[dict] = []
19	_loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
20	
21	
22	def _load_config() -> dict:
23	    if not MCP_CONFIG_PATH.exists():
24	        log.info("No mcp.json found, skipping MCP")
25	        return {"servers": {}}
26	    with open(MCP_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
27	        config = json.load(f)
28	    venv_bin = str(ARGUS_HOME / "venv" / "bin")
29	    for server in config.get("servers", {}).values():
30	        # Substitute ARGUS_VENV in command
31	        if "ARGUS_VENV" in server.get("command", ""):
32	            server["command"] = server["command"].replace("ARGUS_VENV", str(ARGUS_HOME / "venv"))
33	        # Substitute ${ENV_VAR} references in env
34	        env = server.get("env", {})
35	        for k, v in env.items():
36	            if isinstance(v, str) and v.startswith("${") and v.endswith("}"):
37	                env_name = v[2:-1]
38	                env[k] = os.environ.get(env_name, "")
39	    return config
40	
41	
42	def _mcp_schema_to_openai(tool) -> dict:
43	    """Convert an MCP tool schema to OpenAI function-calling format."""
44	    params = {"type": "object", "properties": {}}
45	    if tool.inputSchema:
46	        params = tool.inputSchema
47	    return {
48	        "type": "function",
49	        "function": {
50	            "name": tool.name,
51	            "description": tool.description or "",
52	            "parameters": params,
53	        },
54	        "_mcp_server": "",  # filled in by caller
55	    }
56	
57	
58	async def _run_servers(config: dict):
59	    """Long-running async task that keeps MCP server connections alive."""
60	    import anyio
61	
62	    async with anyio.create_task_group() as tg:
63	        for name, server_config in config.get("servers", {}).items():
64	            tg.start_soon(_run_server, name, server_config)
65	        # This blocks forever, keeping connections alive
66	
67	
68	async def _run_server(name: str, server_config: dict):
69	    """Connect to one MCP server and keep it alive."""
70	    params = StdioServerParameters(
71	        command=server_config["command"],
72	        args=server_config.get("args", []),
73	        env={**os.environ, **server_config.get("env", {})},
74	    )
75	    allowed = set(server_config.get("allowed_tools", []))
76	
77	    async with stdio_client(params) as (read_stream, write_stream):
78	        async with ClientSession(read_stream, write_stream) as session:
79	            await session.initialize()
80	            _sessions[name] = session
81	
82	            tools = await session.list_tools()
83	            count = 0
84	            for tool in tools.tools:
85	                if allowed and tool.name not in allowed:
86	                    continue
87	                openai_tool = _mcp_schema_to_openai(tool)
88	                openai_tool["_mcp_server"] = name
89	                _tools_cache.append(openai_tool)
90	                count += 1
91	
92	            log.info(f"MCP [{name}]: connected, {count}/{len(tools.tools)} tools (filtered)")
93	
94	            # Keep alive forever
95	            while True:
96	                await asyncio.sleep(3600)
97	
98	
99	def start():
100	    """Start MCP servers in a background thread with its own event loop."""
101	    config = _load_config()
102	    if not config.get("servers"):
103	        return
104	
105	    global _loop
106	
107	    def run_loop():
108	        global _loop
109	        _loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
110	        asyncio.set_event_loop(_loop)
111	        try:
112	            _loop.run_until_complete(_run_servers(config))
113	        except Exception as e:
114	            log.error(f"MCP event loop error: {e}")
115	
116	    thread = Thread(target=run_loop, daemon=True)
117	    thread.start()
118	
119	    # Wait for servers to connect (up to 15 seconds)
120	    import time
121	    for _ in range(30):
122	        time.sleep(0.5)
123	        if _sessions:
124	            break
125	
126	    log.info(f"MCP: {len(_tools_cache)} tools loaded from {len(_sessions)} servers")
127	
128	
129	def get_tools() -> list[dict]:
130	    """Return cached MCP tools in OpenAI format."""
131	    return _tools_cache
132	
133	
134	def is_mcp_tool(tool_name: str) -> bool:
135	    """Check if a tool name belongs to an MCP server."""
136	    return any(t["function"]["name"] == tool_name for t in _tools_cache)
137	
138	
139	async def _async_call_tool(tool_name: str, args: dict) -> str:
140	    """Internal async implementation of call_tool."""
141	    server_name = None
142	    for t in _tools_cache:
143	        if t["function"]["name"] == tool_name:
144	            server_name = t.get("_mcp_server")
145	            break
146	
147	    if not server_name or server_name not in _sessions:
148	        return f"Error: MCP tool '{tool_name}' not found"
149	
150	    session = _sessions[server_name]
151	    result = await session.call_tool(tool_name, args)
152	    parts = []
153	    for block in result.content:
154	        if hasattr(block, "text"):
155	            parts.append(block.text)
156	    text = "\n".join(parts) if parts else "(empty result)"
157	    # Truncate large MCP results to prevent context overflow
158	    if len(text) > 4000:
159	        text = text[:4000] + "\n\n... (truncated, result too large)"
160	    # Append task link for task-related tools
161	    if tool_name in ("create_task", "update_task", "complete_task"):
162	        try:
163	            data = json.loads(parts[0]) if parts else {}
164	            task_id = data.get("id")
165	            if task_id:
166	                text += f"\n{VIKUNJA_WEB_URL}/tasks/{task_id}"
167	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, IndexError):
168	            pass
169	    return text
170	
171	
172	def call_tool(tool_name: str, args: dict) -> str:
173	    """Call an MCP tool synchronously (thread-safe)."""
174	    if not _loop:
175	        return f"Error: MCP not running"
176	    try:
177	        future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
178	            _async_call_tool(tool_name, args), _loop
179	        )
180	        return future.result(timeout=30)
181	    except Exception as e:
182	        return f"Error calling {tool_name}: {e}"
183

  "old_string": "# Multi-User Isolation for Argus\n\n## Context\n\nSumeet is inviting Ashlyn (girlfriend) to collaborate on Argus. She's already been added to the GitHub repo. The goal is to let her use the Telegram bot for shared features (task management on Project Pyari) while keeping Sumeet's personal data completely isolated — vault/notes, journal, Strava/fitness, conversation history, system diagnostics.\n\nThe system is currently single-user by design. There's no user_id threading, no per-user conversation history, and no tool access control. Adding Ashlyn to `ALLOWED_USERS` today would give her full access to everything.\n\n## Approach\n\nSimple role-based gating: `admin` (Sumeet, full access) vs `member` (Ashlyn, restricted to shared task management). No abstract frameworks — just if/else on a role string threaded through the existing call chain.\n\n## Changes (7 files + 1 new file)\n\n### 1. `argus/src/config.py` — User roles and permissions\n\nReplace flat `ALLOWED_USERS` set with a `USERS` dict mapping Telegram IDs to role+name. Parse from new env var format `TELEGRAM_USERS=12345:admin:Sumeet,67890:member:Ashlyn`.\n\nAdd constants:\n- `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` — set of tool names blocked for members: `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `search_vault`, `list_files`, `get_strava`, `run_healthcheck`, `run_audit`, `run_investigation`, `git_push_vault`\n- `SHARED_PROJECTS` — `{5}` (Project Pyari)\n- Helper: `get_user(telegram_id) -> dict | None`\n\nKeep `ALLOWED_USERS = set(USERS.keys())` for backward compat with `is_authorized()`.\n\n### 2. `argus/src/db.py` — Per-user conversation history\n\n- Add `user_id INTEGER` column to messages table\n- Auto-migrate existing table with `ALTER TABLE` if column missing\n- Update `log_message(role, content, interface, user_id=None)`\n- Update `get_history(limit, user_id=None)` — filter by user_id when provided\n- Update `clear_history(user_id=None)` — scope deletion to user when provided\n\n### 3. `argus/src/context.py` — Per-role system prompts\n\n- `build_chat_prompt(role=\"admin\")` — loads `chat.md` for admin, `chat_member.md` for member\n- `build_worker_prompt(task, intent, role=\"admin\")` — appends a restriction notice for non-admin users telling the worker to only manage tasks on shared projects\n\n### 4. `argus/prompts/chat_member.md` — New file: member system prompt\n\nStripped-down version of `chat.md`:\n- Same changes:**\n- `chat(user_message, user_id=None, on_status=None)`\n- `run_worker(task, intent, user_id=None, on_status=None)`\n- `_handle_tool_call(name, args, role=\"admin\")`\n\n**New helpers:**\n- `_user_role(user_id)` — returns `\"admin\"` or `\"member\"` from `USERS` config\n- `_filter_tools_for_role(tools, role)` — removes admin-only tools from tool list for members\n\n**Tool gating (defense in depth):**\n- Filter tool list before sending to LLM (member never sees admin tools)\n- Check role again at execution time in `_handle_tool_call` (blocks even if LLM hallucinates a restricted tool)\n- For `do_work`: restrict member's intent enum to `[\"tasks\"]` only (no vault, validation, knowledge, system)\n- For MCP tools: validate `project_id` in args against `SHARED_PROJECTS` for create/update/complete/delete/move operations\n\n**Conversation isolation:**\n- Pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `get_history()` throughout\n\n### 6. `argus/src/telegram_bot.py` — Thread user_id + command gating\n\n- Import `get_user` from config\n- Add `_is_admin(update)` helper\n- `handle_message`: pass `user_id=update.effective_user.id` to `chat()`\n- `_send_and_log`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()`\n- `_run_worker_command`: pass `user_id` to `log_message()` and `run_worker()`\n- `handle_fresh`: pass `user_id` to `clear_history()` (scoped to user)\n- Gate `/health`, `/audit`,   "new_string": "# Multi-User Fixes: Task Filtering + Admin Awareness\n\n## Context\n\nMulti-user role system was deployed. Testing revealed two issues:\n\n1. **Ashlyn can see all tasks across all projects** — `list_all_tasks`, `task_summary`, `overdue_tasks`, etc. return unfiltered results from Vikunja. ## Approved Plan:
# Multi-User Fixes: Task Filtering + Admin Awareness

## Context

Multi-user role system was deployed. Testing revealed two issues:

1. **Ashlyn can see all tasks across all projects** — `list_all_tasks`, `task_summary`, `overdue_tasks`, etc. return unfiltered results from Vikunja. The `_mcp_call_with_project_filter()` in agent.py only gates `project_id` in args (for creates) and `get_task` responses, but listing tools return all projects' data.

2. **Sumeet's admin prompt doesn't know about multi-user** — `chat.md` still reads as if Argus is single-user. It should know Ashlyn is a member user, what the roles mean, and the admin capabilities.

## Fix 1: Enforce task filtering for member MCP results (`agent.py`)

The current `_mcp_call_with_project_filter` at line 63-80 needs to also filter **output** from listing tools. The MCP returns JSON text from Vikunja. We need to:

- For listing tools (`list_all_tasks`, `list_tasks`, `task_summary`, `focus_now`, `due_today`, `overdue_tasks`, `list_projects`), parse the JSON result and filter to only items where `project_id` is in `SHARED_PROJECTS`
- For `list_projects`, filter to only projects whose `id` is in `SHARED_PROJECTS`
- Keep existing input gating (project_id in args) for write operations
- Keep existing `get_task` response check
- Try/except around JSON parsing — if it fails, return a generic "no results" rather than leaking unfiltered data

**Also gate these tools for members:**
- `create_project`, `update_project`, `delete_project` — add to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` in config.py
- `move_task_to_project`, `move_task_to_project_by_name` — need project filtering too

### Changes in `agent.py` `_mcp_call_with_project_filter()`:

```python
LISTING_TOOLS = {"list_all_tasks", "list_tasks", "task_summary", "focus_now", "due_today", "overdue_tasks"}

def _mcp_call_with_project_filter(name: str, args: dict) -> str:
    # Block write ops on non-shared projects  
    if "project_id" in args and args["project_id"] not in SHARED_PROJECTS:
        return "Error: you can only manage tasks on shared projects."

    result = mcp_client.call_tool(name, args)

    # Filter listing results to shared projects only
    if name in LISTING_TOOLS or name == "list_projects":
        try:
            data = json.loads(result)
            if isinstance(data, list):
                key = "id" if name == "list_projects" else "project_id"
                filtered = [item for item in data if item.get(key) in SHARED_PROJECTS]
                return json.dumps(filtered, indent=2) if filtered else "No tasks found."
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return "No results found."

    # Check get_task response
    if name == "get_task":
        try:
            data = json.loads(result)
            if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("project_id") not in SHARED_PROJECTS:
                return "Error: you can only view tasks on shared projects."
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass

    return result
```

### Changes in `config.py`:

Add to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS`:
```python
"create_project", "update_project", "delete_project"
```

## Fix 2: Update admin prompt (`prompts/chat.md`)

Add a section to `chat.md` making Argus aware of the multi-user system. Add after the "WHO YOU ARE" section:

```markdown
## MULTI-USER

Argus is now shared. You have two users:
- **Sumeet** (admin): full access — vault, tasks, Strava, journal, system commands
- **Ashlyn** (member): shared task management only (Project Pyari)

You are currently talking to Sumeet (admin). You have full access to all tools and projects.

Ashlyn is Sumeet's partner. When Sumeet mentions Ashlyn in task contexts, you can reference her Vikunja user (id=2) for task assignments on shared projects.
```

## Files to modify

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `argus/src/agent.py` | Rewrite `_mcp_call_with_project_filter()` to filter listing tool output |
| `argus/src/config.py` | Add `create_project`, `update_project`, `delete_project` to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` |
| `argus/prompts/chat.md` | Add MULTI-USER section after WHO YOU ARE |

## Verification

1. Syntax check: `python -m py_compile argus/src/agent.py`
2. Deploy to VPS, restart argus
3. Test as Ashlyn: `list_all_tasks` / "what's on the board" should only show Project Pyari tasks
4. Test as Sumeet: everything works as before, Argus knows about multi-user setup

```
