# Phase 2: Workout Logging System

## Context

Building a workout system where Argus (Telegram) generates workout plans and fit.argbox.net is the workout card for logging. The flow:

1. `/workout` on Telegram → chat with Argus about equipment, focus, etc.
2. Argus generates a plan, saves to DB, sends link: `fit.argbox.net/workout/42`
3. User taps link → mobile-first workout card with exercises, targets, input boxes
4. User fills in actual reps/duration between sets, adds notes
5. Can add/remove exercises with +/x buttons
6. Auto-saves + manual save button
7. "Finish Workout" marks session complete

No chat on website. All AI stays in Argus. The web page is just a form/tracker.

## Schema Design

### New tables (add to `init_db()` in fitness_db.py)

**`workouts`** — session container:
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workouts (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    date TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    status TEXT DEFAULT 'planned',
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    completed_at TEXT
);
```

**`workout_exercises`** — planned exercises (the AI-generated template):
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workout_exercises (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    workout_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES workouts(id),
    exercise_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES exercises(id),
    position INTEGER NOT NULL,
    variation TEXT DEFAULT 'standard',
    target_sets INTEGER,
    target_reps INTEGER,
    target_duration_seconds INTEGER,
    target_rpe REAL,
    target_weight REAL,
    superset_group INTEGER,
    notes TEXT,
    rest_seconds INTEGER
);
```

**Replace `sets` table** — actual performed sets (linked to planned exercises):
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sets (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    workout_exercise_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES workout_exercises(id),
    set_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    reps INTEGER,
    duration_seconds INTEGER,
    weight REAL,
    rpe REAL,
    notes TEXT,
    logged_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    UNIQUE(workout_exercise_id, set_number)
);
```

**Relationship:** `workouts` 1→N `workout_exercises` 1→N `sets`, with `exercises` as the canonical name catalog.

### Migration
The existing `sets` table is empty (fitness.db is unused), so we can safely drop and recreate. Add migration block after `executescript` that checks schema and handles it.

## Changes by File

### 1. `argus/src/fitness_db.py` — Schema + DB functions

**Schema:** Add `workouts` and `workout_exercises` tables to `init_db()`. Replace `sets` DDL. Add migration for existing DBs.

**New functions:**
- `get_or_create_exercise(name, muscle_group, type)` → exercise_id. Normalizes name (lowercase, strip plurals, hyphens).
- `create_workout_with_exercises(title, exercises_list, date, notes)` → workout_id. Atomic: creates workout + all planned exercises. This is what the Argus tool calls.
- `get_workout(workout_id)` → dict with full workout detail (exercises + logged sets).
- `log_set(workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes)` → set_id. Uses INSERT OR REPLACE on UNIQUE(workout_exercise_id, set_number).
- `log_sets_bulk(sets_data)` → count. Batch version in single transaction.
- `add_exercise_to_workout(workout_id, exercise_name, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, notes)` → workout_exercise_id. Auto-calculates position.
- `remove_exercise_from_workout(workout_exercise_id)` → bool. Deletes exercise + its sets.
- `update_workout_status(workout_id, status)` — sets completed_at if status=completed.
- `list_workouts(limit)` → list of recent workouts for index page.
- `log_measurement(type, value, unit)` → str. Inserts into measurements table.

### 2. `argus/fitness_web/app.py` — Flask routes

**New routes (all use app.py's own `get_db()` to avoid importing config chain):**
- `GET /workout/<id>` — renders `workout.html` with full workout detail
- `POST /workout/<id>/log` — JSON API, accepts `{sets: [...]}`, calls log_sets_bulk. Auto-save endpoint.
- `POST /workout/<id>/exercise` — JSON API, adds exercise to workout, returns new exercise data
- `DELETE /workout/<id>/exercise/<eid>` — JSON API, removes exercise + its sets
- `POST /workout/<id>/status` — JSON API, marks workout completed

**Update `GET /`** — add workouts section above activities list.

**New helper:** `format_target(ex)` — returns human-readable target like "3 x 8 @ RPE 7" or "3 x 60s hold".

### 3. `argus/fitness_web/templates/workout.html` — Mobile-first workout card

- Extends `base.html`
- Header: title + date + status badge
- Exercise cards: name, variation badge, target display, set input rows, notes textarea, x button
- Each set row: set number + input fields (reps or duration, weight if applicable, RPE). Placeholders show targets.
- Superset grouping: exercises with same `superset_group` wrapped in `.superset-block` with orange left border
- `+ Add Exercise` button → bottom sheet modal (exercise name, variation dropdown, sets, reps/duration)
- Save Progress button + Finish Workout button
- Save status indicator ("Saved" / "Saving..." / "Unsaved changes")
- Inputs: `inputmode="numeric"` for number pad on mobile, min-height 44px for tap targets

**JavaScript (inline):**
- Debounced auto-save (800ms) on any input change → POST to `/workout/<id>/log`
- Manual save button → same endpoint
- Add exercise → POST to `/workout/<id>/exercise`, append card to DOM
- Remove exercise → DELETE, remove card from DOM with confirm
- Finish workout → save first, then POST status=completed

### 4. `argus/fitness_web/static/style.css` — Workout styles

- `.exercise-card` — white bg, rounded border, 1rem padding
- `.exercise-header` — flex with name, target, x button
- `.variation-badge` — gray pill for non-standard variations
- `.set-row` — flex with inputs, 44px min-height for mobile
- `.superset-block` — orange left border, grouped padding
- `.add-exercise-btn` — full width, dashed border, "+ Add Exercise"
- `.workout-actions` — flex with save + finish buttons
- `.modal` — fixed overlay, bottom sheet on mobile
- All inputs: centered text, focus border orange (#fc4c02)

### 5. `argus/fitness_web/templates/index.html` — Add workouts section

Add a "Workouts" table above the existing Activities table showing recent workouts (date, title, status badge, click-through to `/workout/<id>`).

### 6. `argus/src/tools.py` — `create_workout` tool

**Tool definition:**
- `title`: string (required)
- `exercises`: array of {name, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, target_rpe, target_weight, superset_group, notes, rest_seconds, muscle_group}
- `notes`: string (optional)

**Execution:** calls `fitness_db.create_workout_with_exercises()`, returns `"Workout created: https://fit.argbox.net/workout/{id}"`

Also add `log_measurement` tool (type, value, unit).

### 7. `argus/src/telegram_bot.py` — `/workout` command

`/workout` is NOT a worker command. It goes through normal `chat()` flow. The user chats naturally about what they want, Argus has the `create_workout` tool, and when ready it creates the plan and sends the link.

```python
async def handle_workout(update, context):
    # Extract any text after /workout as initial message
    user_msg = update.message.text.replace("/workout", "").strip()
    if not user_msg:
        user_msg = "I want to do a workout. Ask me what equipment I have and what I want to focus on."
    # Route through normal chat() — which has create_workout tool
    ...
```

Register in `run()` and `post_init()`.

### 8. `argus/prompts/chat.md` — Add workout tool guidance

Add to TOOLS section:
```
**create_workout**: Create a workout plan. When Sumeet says /workout or wants to plan a workout, chat about equipment/focus/time, then create the workout with exercises and targets based on his goals and level. Send the link.
```

### 9. `argus/src/config.py` — Admin-only gating

Add `create_workout` and `log_measurement` to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS`.

## Implementation order

1. `fitness_db.py` — schema + all DB functions
2. `fitness_web/app.py` — Flask routes (depends on 1 for schema)
3. `fitness_web/templates/workout.html` + CSS — the workout card UI
4. `fitness_web/templates/index.html` — add workouts section
5. `tools.py` — create_workout tool (depends on 1)
6. `telegram_bot.py` — /workout command (depends on 5)
7. `prompts/chat.md` + `config.py` — prompt update + gating

## Verification

1. `python -m py_compile` on all modified files
2. Deploy to VPS, restart argus + fitness_web
3. Test Argus: `/workout` → chat about equipment → Argus creates plan → sends link
4. Test web: open link → see exercises with targets → fill in reps → auto-saves → finish
5. Test add/remove exercise on web page
6. Verify Ashlyn can't access create_workout tool

# Baseline Fitness Test

## Date: 2026-05-08

## Body Composition
- Weight (kg): 75.3
- Body fat % (smart scale): 24.1%
- Height: 170cm

## Strength Tests

### Pull-ups (dead hang, full range of motion, no kipping)
- Max reps in one set: 1.5
- Notes: Could not complete second rep

### Push-ups (strict form — chest to floor, full lockout, no sagging)
- Max reps in one set: 11
- Notes: Good form throughout

### Plank hold (forearm plank, straight line from head to heels)
- Max time: 58 seconds

### Dead hang (bar, overhand grip, full hang)
- Max time: 30 seconds

### Wall handstand hold (back to wall, arms locked out)
- Max time: 15 seconds
- Can you kick up to it? No — walks up only

### Bodyweight squats (full depth, heels down)
- Max reps in one set: 43 (gave up, could push more)
- Notes: Good pace, good depth

### L-sit (on floor with hands beside hips)
- Max hold time: 1.5 seconds
- Position: fully bent knees, feet barely off ground
- Notes: Very weak, major area to develop

### Ring hold (arms locked out at top)
- Max time: 21 seconds

### Ring dips
- Max reps: 0 today (was 2-3 a few months ago)

## Cardio Tests

### 5K time trial
- Distance: 3.91km (not a full 5K)
- Time: 26:19
- Average pace: 6:43/km
- Notes: First run in a while, multiple stops at crossings

### Resting heart rate (morning)
- BPM: 89
- Notes: High — target should be sub-70 with consistent training

## Flexibility Tests

### Toe touch (standing, straight legs)
- Result: Cannot touch toes

### Deep squat hold (heels down, arms in front)
- Max time comfortable: Very long, no issue
- Heels down? Yes

### Shoulder mobility (clasp hands behind back — one arm over, one under)
- Right arm over: Hands clasp (good)
- Left arm over: Cannot reach (imbalance)

## Summary

### Strengths
- Lower body: good squat depth and endurance (43 reps, deep squat hold easy)
- Core: decent plank hold (58s)
- Flexibility: excellent hip/ankle mobility (deep squat)

### Weaknesses
- Pull-ups: 1.5 reps — upper body pulling very weak
- L-sit: 1.5 seconds — core compression strength nearly zero
- Dead hang: 30 seconds — grip strength needs work
- Resting HR: 89 — cardiovascular fitness is poor, need consistent cardio
- Cannot touch toes — hamstring flexibility is poor
- Shoulder mobility imbalance — left side restricted
- Ring hold only 21s — shoulder stability needs building before ring skills

### Focus areas for Phase 1
- Build pulling strength (negatives, band-assisted pull-ups)
- Grip endurance (dead hangs, farmer carries)
- Hamstring flexibility (daily stretching)
- Consistent running to bring resting HR down
- L-sit progression (tucked holds, leg raises)
- Shoulder mobility work (especially left side)

# Fitness Goals

2. Cardio — Running

- Get to around 12% body fat
- Build visible muscle, especially biceps
- Look lean and athletic

### Strength — Intermediate Targets
- Pull-ups: 10-14 strict reps (currently 1.5)
- Push-ups: 35-45 strict reps (currently 11)
- Pistol squat
- Handstand push-ups
- Train confidently on gymnastics rings (dips, push-ups, rows)
- Plank: 2+ minutes (currently 58s)
- L-sit: 15+ seconds full (currently 1.5s tucked)
- Dead hang: 60+ seconds (currently 30s)

### Running — Intermediate Targets
- 5K: sub-23:00 at ~4:30/km pace (currently ~33:00 estimated)
- 10K: sub-48:00 at ~4:40/km pace (not yet attempted)
- Make running fun, not something I hate every time

### Flexibility — Yoga
- Touch my toes (currently cannot)
- Full range of motion in all movements
- Comfortable deep squat (already achieved)
- Good shoulder mobility for ring and handstand work (left side currently restricted)
- Practice proper yoga — not just stretching but a real practice rooted in tradition
- Daily flexibility and mobility work

### Endurance
- Improve stamina and endurance for sex

## Equipment
- At home (2-3 days/week): pull-up bar + gymnastics rings
- Away (2-3 days/week): bodyweight only
- If I have the pull-up bar, I have the rings

## Principles
- No silly exercises — compound movements that drive results
- Progressive overload — get slightly better each session
- Sustainable — this is a lifestyle, not a 12-week challenge
- Train smart — quality over quantity
- Consistency over intensity
- Exercise first, dial in nutrition later

> DEVELOPER

So should the stretch be daily or what? WHat do you think?

> AGENT

From the research, daily short stretching beats less frequent longer sessions. Here's what I'd recommend:

**Every day (non-negotiable):**
- The 15-min mobility routine — post-workout on training days, standalone on rest days. This is what moves the needle on your hamstrings, shoulder, and general flexibility.

**2x/week on rest days:**
- The longer 30-45 min yin session. This is where you get deep flexibility gains — the 3-5 min passive holds that target connective tissue.

**Before every calisthenics session:**
- 3-5 rounds Surya Namaskar + wrist warm-up + shoulder prep. This is warm-up, not stretching — dynamic movement only, no static holds.

So in practice a typical week might look like:

| Day | Training | Flexibility |
|-----|----------|-------------|
| Mon | Strength | Surya Namaskar warm-up + 15 min cool-down stretches |
| Tue | Run | 15 min mobility routine (evening) |
| Wed | Strength | Surya Namaskar warm-up + 15 min cool-down stretches |
| Thu | Rest | 30-45 min yin yoga session |
| Fri | Strength | Surya Namaskar warm-up + 15 min cool-down stretches |
| Sat | Run | 15 min mobility routine (evening) |
| Sun | Rest | 30-45 min yin yoga session |

The daily 15 min is the minimum. The yin sessions are the bonus. You're stretching every single day but it's never a burden — it's either baked into the warm-up/cool-down or it's a chill rest day activity.

That's already in the program.md as written. Want to change anything about it?

> DEVELOPER

What about morning stretches or what?

> AGENT

Good question. The research actually says morning is great for building the habit, even though your body is stiffer. Here's how I'd think about it:

**Morning (5-10 min):** A gentle wake-up flow. Not deep stretching — your muscles are cold. More like:
- Cat-cow
- Downward dog
- Low lunge each side
- Standing forward fold
- Nadi Shodhana breathing (2-3 min)

This is grounding for Vata (loves morning routine), gets blood moving, and sets the tone. It's basically a mini version of the daily routine — not a replacement for it.

**Post-workout (10-15 min):** The full mobility routine. Muscles are warm, you'll get deeper into stretches. This is where the actual flexibility gains happen.

**Evening rest days (30-45 min):** The yin session. Body is most flexible in the evening, and it promotes sleep.

So it stacks:
- **Training days:** Morning flow (5-10 min) + Surya Namaskar warm-up + post-workout stretches (15 min)
- **Rest days:** Morning flow (5-10 min) + yin session in the evening (30-45 min)
- **Lazy days:** Just the morning flow (5-10 min) — still did something

The morning flow is optional but honestly for your Pitta-Vata constitution it's probably the most impactful habit. Calms aggravated Pitta, grounds Vata, and you start the day having already done something for yourself.

Want me to add a morning flow section to the program?

> DEVELOPER

Okay but then like how does that go with the other stretches? I dont wanna have to do too much in a day you know

> AGENT

Yeah fair point. Let's simplify. You don't want 3 separate stretch sessions in a day. How about:

**Training days:** Surya Namaskar warm-up → train → cool-down stretches (15 min). That's it. The stretching is built into the workout, not a separate thing.

**Rest days:** Morning flow (15-20 min) — the full mobility routine. This is your one thing for the day. No yin session on top of it unless you feel like it.

So on any given day, you're doing **one stretch block**, never more:

| Day type | When | What | Duration |
|----------|------|------|----------|
| Training | Around workout | Warm-up + cool-down stretches | ~20 min total (baked in) |
| Rest | Morning | Full mobility routine | 15-20 min |
| Yin bonus | Evening, if you feel like it | Longer yin session | 30-45 min (optional) |

The yin sessions become a "nice to have" rather than a "must do". If you're feeling stiff or want to chill with some deep stretches on a Sunday evening, do it. But it's not prescribed.

That way you're stretching daily but it never feels like an extra chore. Want me to update the program with this?

## Yoga & Flexibility Program

### Daily Mobility Routine (15-20 min, post-workout or morning)

1. Cat-Cow: 10 reps (1 min)
2. Downward Dog (pedal feet): 45s
3. Low Lunge (Anjaneyasana) each side: 45s each
4. Pigeon Pose each side: 60s each
5. Thread the Needle each side: 30s each
6. Seated Forward Fold: 60s
7. Cow Face Arms each side: 30s each (extra time on left)
8. Supine Twist each side: 45s each
9. Butterfly Pose: 60s
10. Standing Forward Fold: 45s
11. Supported Fish Pose (block/towel under t-spine): 2 min
12. Savasana: 2 min

### Dedicated Yin Session (30-45 min, rest days, 2x/week)

1. Surya Namaskar A — 3-5 rounds slow: 8-10 min
2. Warrior I + Warrior II + Triangle each side: 6 min
3. Wide-legged Forward Fold: 90s
4. Pigeon Pose each side: 3 min each (yin hold)
5. Lizard Pose each side: 90s each
6. Seated Forward Fold: 3 min (yin hold)
7. Cow Face Pose (full) each side: 2 min each
8. Supported Fish Pose: 3 min
9. Lying T-Spine Rotation each side: 90s each
10. Supine Hamstring Stretch (strap) each side: 90s each
11. Happy Baby: 90s
12. Savasana with Nadi Shodhana breathing: 5 min

### Warm-up Before Calisthenics

3-5 rounds of Surya Namaskar (slow pace, ~8 min) followed by:
- Wrist warm-up: circles, prayer stretch, kneeling extensions/flexions, fist-to-open (3-5 min)
- Shoulder prep: band pull-aparts (15), wall slides (10), shoulder dislocates (10) — extra on left side
- Hip: 90/90 switches (8 each), Cossack squats (6 each)

### Priority Areas
1. **Hamstrings** — daily forward folds, seated folds, supine stretches with strap. Goal: touch toes in 3-6 months.
2. **Left shoulder** — 1.5x hold time on left for all bilateral shoulder stretches. Cow face arms, thread the needle, puppy pose.
3. **Wrists** — non-negotiable 3-5 min warm-up before every calisthenics session.
4. **Hips** — pigeon pose is the king. Deep squat holds for maintenance.
5. **Thoracic spine** — foam roller extensions, cat-cow, thread the needle.

### Pranayama
- **Daily:** Nadi Shodhana (alternate nostril breathing) — 5-10 min. Balances Pitta-Vata.
- **After hard sessions:** Sheetali (inhale through rolled tongue) — cooling for Pitta.
- **Avoid:** Kapalabhati and Bhastrika (too heating/stimulating for Pitta-Vata).

## GTG Protocol (Greasing the Groove)

54	### 6. Yoga / Flexibility Program
55	- **Daily**: 15 min post-workout mobility routine (specific poses listed)
56	- **2x/week**: 30-45 min dedicated yin yoga sessions on rest days
57	- **Warm-up**: 3-5 rounds Surya Namaskar before calisthenics

61	### 7. GTG Protocol
62	- Pull-ups: when at home, do singles/doubles spread through day (50% of max, 5-8x daily)
63	- Rules: never to failure, every rep crisp, spread 1+ hours apart
64	- Retest max every 2-3 weeks, adjust working reps
65	- Can be flagged when asking for a workout: "I'm home today" → includes GTG
66	
67	### 8. Recovery Rules (for Argus to enforce)
68	- Min 48 hours between hard sessions for same muscle group
69	- If trained 3+ consecutive days → suggest rest day
70	- If 3+ days since last workout → send nudge
71	- Deload every 8-10 weeks (cut volume 40-60%, keep same exercises at easier variations)
72	- Signs to auto-suggest rest: user reports poor sleep, joint pain, or motivation drop
73	
74	### 9. Nutrition Guidelines
75	- Small deficit: 200-300 cal below maintenance (~2000-2200 kcal/day)
76	- Protein: 2.0-2.4g/kg (150-180g/day)
77	- Fat: 0.8-1.0g/kg (60-75g/day)
78	- Carbs: fill remaining
79	- Hydrate well, especially for Pitta constitution
80	
81	### 10. Milestones & Testing
82	- Retest baseline exercises every 4-6 weeks
83	- Track: max pull-ups, max push-ups, plank hold, 5K time, weight, body fat
84	- Milestone targets at 3, 6, 12 months (from research)

98	
99	# Phase 2: Workout Logging System
100	
101	## Context
102	
103	Building a workout system where Argus (Telegram) generates workout plans and fit.argbox.net is the workout card for logging. The flow:
104	
105	1. `/workout` on Telegram → chat with Argus about equipment, focus, etc.
106	2. Argus generates a plan, saves to DB, sends link: `fit.argbox.net/workout/42`
107	3. User taps link → mobile-first workout card with exercises, targets, input boxes
108	4. User fills in actual reps/duration between sets, adds notes
109	5. Can add/remove exercises with +/x buttons
110	6. Auto-saves + manual save button
111	7. "Finish Workout" marks session complete
112	
113	No chat on website. All AI stays in Argus. The web page is just a form/tracker.
114	
115	## Schema Design
116	
117	### New tables (add to `init_db()` in fitness_db.py)
118	
119	**`workouts`** — session container:
120	```sql
121	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workouts (
122	    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
123	    date TEXT NOT NULL,
124	    title TEXT,
125	    notes TEXT,
126	    status TEXT DEFAULT 'planned',
127	    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
128	    completed_at TEXT
129	);
130	```
131	
132	**`workout_exercises`** — planned exercises (the AI-generated template):
133	```sql
134	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workout_exercises (
135	    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
136	    workout_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES workouts(id),
137	    exercise_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES exercises(id),
138	    position INTEGER NOT NULL,
139	    variation TEXT DEFAULT 'standard',
140	    target_sets INTEGER,
141	    target_reps INTEGER,
142	    target_duration_seconds INTEGER,
143	    target_rpe REAL,
144	    target_weight REAL,
145	    superset_group INTEGER,
146	    notes TEXT,
147	    rest_seconds INTEGER
148	);
149	```
150	
151	**Replace `sets` table** — actual performed sets (linked to planned exercises):
152	```sql
153	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sets (
154	    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
155	    workout_exercise_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES workout_exercises(id),
156	    set_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
157	    reps INTEGER,
158	    duration_seconds INTEGER,
159	    weight REAL,
160	    rpe REAL,
161	    notes TEXT,
162	    logged_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
163	    UNIQUE(workout_exercise_id, set_number)
164	);
165	```
166	
167	**Relationship:** `workouts` 1→N `workout_exercises` 1→N `sets`, with `exercises` as the canonical name catalog.
168	
169	### Migration
170	The existing `sets` table is empty (fitness.db is unused), so we can safely drop and recreate. Add migration block after `executescript` that checks schema and handles it.
171	
172	## Changes by File
173	
174	### 1. `argus/src/fitness_db.py` — Schema + DB functions
175	
176	**Schema:** Add `workouts` and `workout_exercises` tables to `init_db()`. Replace `sets` DDL. Add migration for existing DBs.
177	
178	**New functions:**
179	- `get_or_create_exercise(name, muscle_group, type)` → exercise_id. Normalizes name (lowercase, strip plurals, hyphens).
180	- `create_workout_with_exercises(title, exercises_list, date, notes)` → workout_id. Atomic: creates workout + all planned exercises. This is what the Argus tool calls.
181	- `get_workout(workout_id)` → dict with full workout detail (exercises + logged sets).
182	- `log_set(workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes)` → set_id. Uses INSERT OR REPLACE on UNIQUE(workout_exercise_id, set_number).
183	- `log_sets_bulk(sets_data)` → count. Batch version in single transaction.
184	- `add_exercise_to_workout(workout_id, exercise_name, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, notes)` → workout_exercise_id. Auto-calculates position.
185	- `remove_exercise_from_workout(workout_exercise_id)` → bool. Deletes exercise + its sets.
186	- `update_workout_status(workout_id, status)` — sets completed_at if status=completed.
187	- `list_workouts(limit)` → list of recent workouts for index page.
188	- `log_measurement(type, value, unit)` → str. Inserts into measurements table.
189	
190	### 2. `argus/fitness_web/app.py` — Flask routes
191	
192	**New routes (all use app.py's own `get_db()` to avoid importing config chain):**
193	- `GET /workout/<id>` — renders `workout.html` with full workout detail
194	- `POST /workout/<id>/log` — JSON API, accepts `{sets: [...]}`, calls log_sets_bulk. Auto-save endpoint.
195	- `POST /workout/<id>/exercise` — JSON API, adds exercise to workout, returns new exercise data
196	- `DELETE /workout/<id>/exercise/<eid>` — JSON API, removes exercise + its sets
197	- `POST /workout/<id>/status` — JSON API, marks workout completed
198	
199	**Update `GET /`** — add workouts section above activities list.
200	
201	**New helper:** `format_target(ex)` — returns human-readable target like "3 x 8 @ RPE 7" or "3 x 60s hold".
202	
203	### 3. `argus/fitness_web/templates/workout.html` — Mobile-first workout card
204	
205	- Extends `base.html`
206	- Header: title + date + status badge
207	- Exercise cards: name, variation badge, target display, set input rows, notes textarea, x button
208	- Each set row: set number + input fields (reps or duration, weight if applicable, RPE). Placeholders show targets.
209	- Superset grouping: exercises with same `superset_group` wrapped in `.superset-block` with orange left border
210	- `+ Add Exercise` button → bottom sheet modal (exercise name, variation dropdown, sets, reps/duration)
211	- Save Progress button + Finish Workout button
212	- Save status indicator ("Saved" / "Saving..." / "Unsaved changes")
213	- Inputs: `inputmode="numeric"` for number pad on mobile, min-height 44px for tap targets
214	
215	**JavaScript (inline):**
216	- Debounced auto-save (800ms) on any input change → POST to `/workout/<id>/log`
217	- Manual save button → same endpoint
218	- Add exercise → POST to `/workout/<id>/exercise`, append card to DOM
219	- Remove exercise → DELETE, remove card from DOM with confirm
220	- Finish workout → save first, then POST status=completed
221	
222	### 4. `argus/fitness_web/static/style.css` — Workout styles
223	
224	- `.exercise-card` — white bg, rounded border, 1rem padding
225	- `.exercise-header` — flex with name, target, x button
226	- `.variation-badge` — gray pill for non-standard variations
227	- `.set-row` — flex with inputs, 44px min-height for mobile
228	- `.superset-block` — orange left border, grouped padding
229	- `.add-exercise-btn` — full width, dashed border, "+ Add Exercise"
230	- `.workout-actions` — flex with save + finish buttons
231	- `.modal` — fixed overlay, bottom sheet on mobile
232	- All inputs: centered text, focus border orange (#fc4c02)
233	
234	### 5. `argus/fitness_web/templates/index.html` — Add workouts section
235	
236	Add a "Workouts" table above the existing Activities table showing recent workouts (date, title, status badge, click-through to `/workout/<id>`).
237	
238	### 6. `argus/src/tools.py` — `create_workout` tool
239	
240	**Tool definition:**
241	- `title`: string (required)
242	- `exercises`: array of {name, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, target_rpe, target_weight, superset_group, notes, rest_seconds, muscle_group}
243	- `notes`: string (optional)
244	
245	**Execution:** calls `fitness_db.create_workout_with_exercises()`, returns `"Workout created: https://fit.argbox.net/workout/{id}"`
246	
247	Also add `log_measurement` tool (type, value, unit).
248	
249	### 7. `argus/src/telegram_bot.py` — `/workout` command
250	
251	`/workout` is NOT a worker command. It goes through normal `chat()` flow. The user chats naturally about what they want, Argus has the `create_workout` tool, and when ready it creates the plan and sends the link.
252	
253	```python
254	async def handle_workout(update, context):
255	    # Extract any text after /workout as initial message
256	    user_msg = update.message.text.replace("/workout", "").strip()
257	    if not user_msg:
258	        user_msg = "I want to do a workout. Ask me what equipment I have and what I want to focus on."
259	    # Route through normal chat() — which has create_workout tool
260	    ...
261	```
262	
263	Register in `run()` and `post_init()`.
264	
265	### 8. `argus/prompts/chat.md` — Add workout tool guidance
266	
267	Add to TOOLS section:
268	```
269	**create_workout**: Create a workout plan. When Sumeet says /workout or wants to plan a workout, chat about equipment/focus/time, then create the workout with exercises and targets based on his goals and level. Send the link.
270	```
271	
272	### 9. `argus/src/config.py` — Admin-only gating
273	
274	Add `create_workout` and `log_measurement` to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS`.
275	
276	## Implementation order
277	
278	1. `fitness_db.py` — schema + all DB functions
279	2. `fitness_web/app.py` — Flask routes (depends on 1 for schema)
280	3. `fitness_web/templates/workout.html` + CSS — the workout card UI
281	4. `fitness_web/templates/index.html` — add workouts section
282	5. `tools.py` — create_workout tool (depends on 1)
283	6. `telegram_bot.py` — /workout command (depends on 5)
284	7. `prompts/chat.md` + `config.py` — prompt update + gating
285	
286	## Verification
287	
288	1. `python -m py_compile` on all modified files
289	2. Deploy to VPS, restart argus + fitness_web
290	3. Test Argus: `/workout` → chat about equipment → Argus creates plan → sends link
291	4. Test web: open link → see exercises with targets → fill in reps → auto-saves → finish
292	5. Test add/remove exercise on web page
293	6. Verify Ashlyn can't access create_workout tool
294

1	"""Fitness database — SQLite storage for workouts, measurements, and stats."""
2	
3	import json
4	import sqlite3
5	from pathlib import Path
6	
7	from .config import ARGUS_HOME, log
8	
9	DB_PATH = ARGUS_HOME / "data" / "fitness.db"
10	
11	
12	def get_db() -> sqlite3.Connection:
13	    DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
14	    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH))
15	    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
16	    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
17	    return conn
18	
19	
20	def init_db():
21	    """Create tables if they don't exist."""
22	    conn = get_db()
23	    conn.executescript("""
24	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activities (
25	            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

157	def activity_exists(strava_id: int) -> bool:
158	    conn = get_db()
159	    row = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM activities WHERE strava_id = ?", (strava_id,)).fetchone()
160	    conn.close()
161	    return row is not None
162	
163	
164	def store_strava_activity(activity: dict, detail: dict) -> int:
165	    """Store a Strava activity with its detail data. Returns the local activity ID."""
166	    conn = get_db()
167	
168	    conn.execute("""
169	        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO activities (
170	            strava_id, date, name, type, sport_type, distance,
171	            moving_time, elapsed_time, average_speed, max_speed,
172	            total_elevation_gain, elev_high, elev_low,
173	            average_heartrate, max_heartrate, average_cadence,
174	            calories, suffer_score, perceived_exertion, pr_count,
175	            device_name, start_latlng, end_latlng, summary_polyline, source
176	        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'strava')
177	    """, (
178	        detail["id"],
179	        detail["start_date_local"][:10],
180	        detail.get("name"),
181	        detail.get("type"),
182	        detail.get("sport_type"),
183	        detail.get("distance"),
184	        detail.get("moving_time"),
185	        detail.get("elapsed_time"),
186	        detail.get("average_speed"),
187	        detail.get("max_speed"),
188	        detail.get("total_elevation_gain"),
189	        detail.get("elev_high"),
190	        detail.get("elev_low"),
191	        detail.get("average_heartrate"),
192	        detail.get("max_heartrate"),
193	        detail.get("average_cadence"),
194	        detail.get("calories"),
195	        detail.get("suffer_score"),
196	        detail.get("perceived_exertion"),
197	        detail.get("pr_count"),
198	        detail.get("device_name"),
199	        str(detail.get("start_latlng", "")),
200	        str(detail.get("end_latlng", "")),
201	        detail.get("map", {}).get("summary_polyline", ""),
202	    ))
203	
204	    local_id = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM activities WHERE strava_id = ?", (detail["id"],)).fetchone()["id"]
205	
206	    # Splits
207	    for split in detail.get("splits_metric", []):
208	        conn.execute("""
209	            INSERT INTO splits (
210	                activity_id, split_number, distance, moving_time, elapsed_time,
211	                average_speed, average_grade_adjusted_speed, elevation_difference,
212	                average_heartrate, pace_zone
213	            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
214	        """, (
215	            local_id,
216	            split.get("split"),
217	            split.get("distance"),
218	            split.get("moving_time"),
219	            split.get("elapsed_time"),
220	            split.get("average_speed"),
221	            split.get("average_grade_adjusted_speed"),
222	            split.get("elevation_difference"),
223	            split.get("average_heartrate"),
224	            split.get("pace_zone"),
225	        ))
226	
227	    # Laps
228	    for lap in detail.get("laps", []):
229	        conn.execute("""
230	            INSERT INTO laps (
231	                activity_id, lap_index, name, distance, moving_time, elapsed_time,
232	                average_speed, max_speed, average_heartrate, max_heartrate,
233	                average_cadence, total_elevation_gain, pace_zone
234	            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
235	        """, (
236	            local_id,
237	            lap.get("lap_index"),
238	            lap.get("name"),
239	            lap.get("distance"),
240	            lap.get("moving_time"),
241	            lap.get("elapsed_time"),
242	            lap.get("average_speed"),
243	            lap.get("max_speed"),
244	            lap.get("average_heartrate"),
245	            lap.get("max_heartrate"),
246	            lap.get("average_cadence"),
247	            lap.get("total_elevation_gain"),
248	            lap.get("pace_zone"),
249	        ))
250	
251	    # Best efforts
252	    for effort in detail.get("best_efforts", []):
253	        conn.execute("""
254	            INSERT INTO best_efforts (
255	                activity_id, name, distance, moving_time, elapsed_time, pr_rank
256	            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
257	        """, (
258	            local_id,
259	            effort.get("name"),
260	            effort.get("distance"),
261	            effort.get("moving_time"),
262	            effort.get("elapsed_time"),
263	            effort.get("pr_rank"),
264	        ))
265	
266	    # Segment efforts
267	    for seg in detail.get("segment_efforts", []):
268	        segment = seg.get("segment", {})
269	        start_ll = segment.get("start_latlng", [None, None])
270	        end_ll = segment.get("end_latlng", [None, None])
271	        conn.execute("""
272	            INSERT INTO segment_efforts (
273	                activity_id, strava_segment_id, name, distance, moving_time,
274	                elapsed_time, start_index, end_index, start_lat, start_lng,
275	                end_lat, end_lng, average_heartrate, pr_rank, hidden
276	            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
277	        """, (
278	            local_id,
279	            segment.get("id"),
280	            seg.get("name"),
281	            seg.get("distance"),
282	            seg.get("moving_time"),
283	            seg.get("elapsed_time"),
284	            seg.get("start_index"),
285	            seg.get("end_index"),
286	            start_ll[0] if start_ll else None,
287	            start_ll[1] if start_ll else None,
288	            end_ll[0] if end_ll else None,
289	            end_ll[1] if end_ll else None,
290	            seg.get("average_heartrate"),
291	            seg.get("pr_rank"),
292	            0,
293	        ))
294	
295	    # Streams
296	    from .strava import _headers
297	    import requests as _req
298	    try:
299	        streams_resp = _req.get(
300	            f"https://www.strava.com/api/v3/activities/{detail['id']}/streams",
301	            headers=_headers(),
302	            params={"keys": "distance,velocity_smooth,altitude,heartrate,cadence,time,latlng,grade_smooth,watts,temp,moving", "key_type": "distance"},
303	            timeout=15,
304	        )
305	        if streams_resp.status_code == 200:
306	            for stream in streams_resp.json():
307	                conn.execute("""
308	                    INSERT OR REPLACE INTO activity_streams (activity_id, stream_type, data)
309	                    VALUES (?, ?, ?)
310	                """, (local_id, stream["type"], json.dumps(stream["data"])))
311	    except Exception as e:
312	        log.warning(f"Failed to fetch streams for {detail['id']}: {e}")
313	
314	    conn.commit()
315	    conn.close()
316	    return local_id
317	
318	
319	def backfill_polylines() -> str:
320	    """Fetch polylines for activities that don't have them yet."""
321	    from .strava import _headers
322	    import requests
323	    conn = get_db()
324	    rows = conn.execute(
325	        "SELECT id, strava_id FROM activities WHERE summary_polyline IS NULL OR summary_polyline = ''"
326	    ).fetchall()
327	    count = 0
328	    for row in rows:
329	        resp = requests.get(
330	            f"https://www.strava.com/api/v3/activities/{row['strava_id']}",
331	            headers=_headers(), timeout=15,
332	        )
333	        resp.raise_for_status()
334	        polyline = resp.json().get("map", {}).get("summary_polyline", "")
335	        if polyline:
336	            conn.execute("UPDATE activities SET summary_polyline = ? WHERE id = ?",
337	                         (polyline, row["id"]))
338	            count += 1
339	    conn.commit()
340	    conn.close()
341	    return f"Backfilled {count} polylines"
342	
343	
344	def backfill_streams() -> str:
345	    """Fetch streams for activities that don't have them yet."""
346	    from .strava import _headers
347	    import requests
348	    conn = get_db()
349	    rows = conn.execute("""
350	        SELECT a.id, a.strava_id FROM activities a
351	        WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM activity_streams s WHERE s.activity_id = a.id)
352	    """).fetchall()
353	    count = 0
354	    for row in rows:
355	        try:
356	            resp = requests.get(
357	                f"https://www.strava.com/api/v3/activities/{row['strava_id']}/streams",
358	                headers=_headers(),
359	                params={"keys": "distance,velocity_smooth,altitude,heartrate,cadence,time,latlng,grade_smooth,watts,temp,moving", "key_type": "distance"},
360	                timeout=15,
361	            )
362	            if resp.status_code == 200:
363	                for stream in resp.json():
364	                    conn.execute("""
365	                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO activity_streams (activity_id, stream_type, data)
366	                        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
367	                    """, (row["id"], stream["type"], json.dumps(stream["data"])))
368	                count += 1
369	        except Exception as e:
370	            log.warning(f"Failed to fetch streams for {row['strava_id']}: {e}")
371	    conn.commit()
372	    conn.close()
373	    return f"Backfilled streams for {count} activities"
374	
375	
376	def sync_strava(count: int = 10) -> str:
377	    """Pull recent Strava activities and store any that aren't already in the DB."""
378	    from .strava import _ensure_token, _headers
379	    import requests
380	
381	    init_db()
382	
383	    resp = requests.get(
384	        "https://www.strava.com/api/v3/athlete/activities",
385	        headers=_headers(),
386	        params={"per_page": count},
387	        timeout=15,
388	    )
389	    resp.raise_for_status()
390	    activities = resp.json()
391	
392	    new_count = 0
393	    synced = []
394	
395	    for summary in activities:
396	        strava_id = summary["id"]
397	        if activity_exists(strava_id):
398	            continue
399	
400	        # Fetch full detail
401	        detail_resp = requests.get(
402	            f"https://www.strava.com/api/v3/activities/{strava_id}",
403	            headers=_headers(),
404	            timeout=15,
405	        )
406	        detail_resp.raise_for_status()
407	        detail = detail_resp.json()
408	
409	        local_id = store_strava_activity(summary, detail)
410	        new_count += 1
411	
412	        dist_km = detail["distance"] / 1000
413	        date = detail["start_date_local"][:10]
414	        synced.append(f"- {date} | {detail['name']} ({detail['type']}) | {dist_km:.2f}km")
415	        log.info(f"Synced Strava activity {strava_id} -> local {local_id}")
416	
417	    if new_count == 0:
418	        return "All Strava activities already synced."
419	
420	    return f"Synced {new_count} new activities:\n" + "\n".join(synced)
421

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/src/fitness_db.py",
  "old_string": "    return f\"Synced {new_count} new activities:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(synced)",
  "new_string": "    return f\"Synced {new_count} new activities:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(synced)\n\n\n# --- Workout functions ---\n\ndef _normalize_exercise(name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Normalize exercise name: lowercase, strip plurals, hyphens for spaces.\"\"\"\n    name = name.lower().strip()\n    name = re.sub(r\"s$\", \"\", name)  # pull-ups -> pull-up\n    name = re.sub(r\"[\\s_]+\", \"-\", name)  # \"push up\" -> \"push-up\"\n    return name\n\n\ndef get_or_create_exercise(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, muscle_group: str | None = None, exercise_type: str = \"strength\") -> int:\n    \"\"\"Get exercise ID by name, creating it if it doesn't exist.\"\"\"\n    normalized = _normalize_exercise(name)\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM exercises WHERE LOWER(name) = ?\", (normalized,)).fetchone()\n    if row:\n        return row[\"id\"]\n    conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO exercises (name, muscle_group, type) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\",\n                 (normalized, muscle_group, exercise_type))\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef create_workout_with_exercises(title: str, exercises: list[dict], date: str | None = None, notes: str | None = None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a workout with planned exercises. Returns the workout ID.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    if not date:\n        date = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n\n    conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO workouts (date, title, notes) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\", (date, title, notes))\n    workout_id = conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n\n    for i, ex in enumerate(exercises):\n        ex_name = ex.get(\"name\", \"unknown\")\n        muscle_group = ex.get(\"muscle_group\")\n        # Infer type from target fields\n        ex_type = \"hold\" if ex.get(\"target_duration_seconds\") else \"strength\"\n        exercise_id = get_or_create_exercise(conn, ex_name, muscle_group, ex_type)\n\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO workout_exercises (\n                workout_id, exercise_id, position, variation,\n                target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds,\n                target_rpe, target_weight, superset_group, notes, rest_seconds\n            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n        \"\"\", (\n            workout_id, exercise_id, i + 1,\n            ex.get(\"variation\", \"standard\"),\n            ex.get(\"target_sets\"),\n            ex.get(\"target_reps\"),\n            ex.get(\"target_duration_seconds\"),\n            ex.get(\"target_rpe\"),\n            ex.get(\"target_weight\"),\n            ex.get(\"superset_group\"),\n            ex.get(\"notes\"),\n            ex.get(\"rest_seconds\"),\n        ))\n\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    log.info(f\"Created workout {workout_id}: {title} with {len(exercises)} exercises\")\n    return workout_id\n\n\ndef get_workout(workout_id: int) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"Get full workout detail: metadata + exercises + logged sets.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    workout = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM workouts WHERE id = ?\", (workout_id,)).fetchone()\n    if not workout:\n        conn.close()\n        return None\n\n    result = dict(workout)\n    result[\"exercises\"] = []\n\n    exercises = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT we.*, e.name as exercise_name\n        FROM workout_exercises we\n        JOIN exercises e ON we.exercise_id = e.id\n        WHERE we.workout_id = ?\n        ORDER BY we.position\n    \"\"\", (workout_id,)).fetchall()\n\n    for ex in exercises:\n        ex_dict = dict(ex)\n        sets = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM sets WHERE workout_exercise_id = ? ORDER BY set_number\n        \"\"\", (ex[\"id\"],)).fetchall()\n        ex_dict[\"sets\"] = [dict(s) for s in sets]\n        result[\"exercises\"].append(ex_dict)\n\n    conn.close()\n    return result\n\n\ndef log_set(workout_exercise_id: int, set_number: int, reps: int | None = None,\n            duration_seconds: int | None = None, weight: float | None = None,\n            rpe: float | None = None, notes: str | None = None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Log or update a single set. Uses INSERT OR REPLACE on the unique constraint.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sets (workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n    \"\"\", (workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes))\n    set_id = conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return set_id\n\n\ndef log_sets_bulk(sets_data: list[dict]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Log multiple sets in a single transaction. Returns count of sets logged.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    count = 0\n    for s in sets_data:\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sets (workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n        \"\"\", (\n            s[\"workout_exercise_id\"], s[\"set_number\"],\n            s.get(\"reps\"), s.get(\"duration_seconds\"),\n            s.get(\"weight\"), s.get(\"rpe\"), s.get(\"notes\"),\n        ))\n        count += 1\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return count\n\n\ndef add_exercise_to_workout(workout_id: int, exercise_name: str, variation: str = \"standard\",\n                            target_sets: int | None = None, target_reps: int | None = None,\n                            target_duration_seconds: int | None = None, notes: str | None = None) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Add a new exercise to an existing workout. Returns the new exercise dict.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    ex_type = \"hold\" if target_duration_seconds else \"strength\"\n    exercise_id = get_or_create_exercise(conn, exercise_name, exercise_type=ex_type)\n\n    # Auto-calculate position\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position), 0) + 1 as pos FROM workout_exercises WHERE workout_id = ?\",\n                       (workout_id,)).fetchone()\n    position = row[\"pos\"]\n\n    conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO workout_exercises (workout_id, exercise_id, position, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, notes)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n    \"\"\", (workout_id, exercise_id, position, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, notes))\n    we_id = conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n\n    # Get the exercise name back for the response\n    result = {\n        \"id\": we_id, \"exercise_name\": _normalize_exercise(exercise_name),\n        \"variation\": variation, \"target_sets\": target_sets, \"target_reps\": target_reps,\n        \"target_duration_seconds\": target_duration_seconds, \"notes\": notes, \"position\": position,\n    }\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return result\n\n\ndef remove_exercise_from_workout(workout_exercise_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Remove a planned exercise and its logged sets from a workout.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM sets WHERE workout_exercise_id = ?\", (workout_exercise_id,))\n    cursor = conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM workout_exercises WHERE id = ?\", (workout_exercise_id,))\n    deleted = cursor.rowcount > 0\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return deleted\n\n\ndef update_workout_status(workout_id: int, status: str):\n    \"\"\"Update workout status. Sets completed_at when status is 'completed'.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    if status == \"completed\":\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE workouts SET status = ?, completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, workout_id))\n    else:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE workouts SET status = ? WHERE id = ?\", (status, workout_id))\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef list_workouts(limit: int = 20) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"List recent workouts with exercise count.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_db()\n    rows = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT w.*, COUNT(we.id) as exercise_count\n        FROM workouts w\n        LEFT JOIN workout_exercises we ON we.workout_id = w.id\n        GROUP BY w.id\n        ORDER BY w.date DESC, w.created_at DESC\n        LIMIT ?\n    \"\"\", (limit,)).fetchall()\n    conn.close()\n    return [dict(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef log_measurement(type: str, value: float, unit: str | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Log a body measurement (weight, body_fat, resting_hr, etc.).\"\"\"\n    if unit is None:\n        unit = {\"weight\": \"kg\", \"body_fat\": \"%\", \"resting_hr\": \"bpm\"}.get(type, \"\")\n    conn = get_db()\n    date = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO measurements (date, type, value, unit) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                 (date, type, value, unit))\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return f\"Logged {type}: {value}{unit}\""
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/fitness_web/app.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Fitness dashboard — web viewer for workout data and workout logging.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.signal import savgol_filter\nfrom flask import Flask, render_template, request, jsonify\n\nDB_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"data\" / \"fitness.db\"\n\napp = Flask(__name__)\n\n\ndef get_db() -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH))\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef format_pace(speed_ms):\n    if not speed_ms or speed_ms <= 0:\n        return \"N/A\"\n    pace_s = 1000 / speed_ms\n    return f\"{int(pace_s // 60)}:{int(pace_s % 60):02d}/km\"\n\n\ndef format_duration(seconds):\n    if not seconds:\n        return \"N/A\"\n    seconds = int(seconds)\n    h, m, s = seconds // 3600, (seconds % 3600) // 60, seconds % 60\n    if h > 0:\n        return f\"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}\"\n    return f\"{m}:{s:02d}\"\n\n\ndef format_target(ex):\n    \"\"\"Format exercise target as human-readable string.\"\"\"\n    parts = []\n    sets = ex.get(\"target_sets\") or ex.get(\"target_sets\")\n    if ex.get(\"target_duration_seconds\"):\n        if sets:\n            parts.append(f\"{sets} x {ex['target_duration_seconds']}s hold\")\n        else:\n            parts.append(f\"{ex['target_duration_seconds']}s hold\")\n    elif ex.get(\"target_reps\"):\n        if sets:\n            parts.append(f\"{sets} x {ex['target_reps']}\")\n        else:\n            parts.append(f\"{ex['target_reps']} reps\")\n    if ex.get(\"target_weight\"):\n        parts.append(f\"@ {ex['target_weight']}kg\")\n    if ex.get(\"target_rpe\"):\n        parts.append(f\"RPE {ex['target_rpe']}\")\n    return \" \".join(parts) if parts else \"\"\n\n\n@app.route(\"/\")\ndef index():\n    conn = get_db()\n    activities = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT id, date, name, type, distance, moving_time,\n               average_speed, total_elevation_gain, average_heartrate, calories\n        FROM activities ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 50\n    \"\"\").fetchall()\n    workouts = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT w.*, COUNT(we.id) as exercise_count\n        FROM workouts w\n        LEFT JOIN workout_exercises we ON we.workout_id = w.id\n        GROUP BY w.id\n        ORDER BY w.date DESC, w.created_at DESC\n        LIMIT 20\n    \"\"\").fetchall()\n    conn.close()\n    return render_template(\"index.html\", activities=activities, workouts=workouts,\n                           format_pace=format_pace, format_duration=format_duration)\n\n\n@app.route(\"/activity/<int:activity_id>\")\ndef activity_detail(activity_id):\n    conn = get_db()\n    activity = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM activities WHERE id = ?\", (activity_id,)).fetchone()\n    if not activity:\n        return \"Activity not found\", 404\n    splits = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM splits WHERE activity_id = ? ORDER BY split_number\",\n        (activity_id,)\n    ).fetchall()\n    best_efforts = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM best_efforts WHERE activity_id = ? ORDER BY distance\",\n        (activity_id,)\n    ).fetchall()\n    laps = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM laps WHERE activity_id = ? ORDER BY lap_index\",\n        (activity_id,)\n    ).fetchall()\n    segments = conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM segment_efforts WHERE activity_id = ? ORDER BY start_index\",\n        (activity_id,)\n    ).fetchall()\n    streams = {}\n    for row in conn.execute(\"SELECT stream_type, data FROM activity_streams WHERE activity_id = ?\", (activity_id,)):\n        streams[row[\"stream_type\"]] = json.loads(row[\"data\"])\n    conn.close()\n\n    # Compute smoothed pace chart data server-side using Savitzky-Golay filter\n    chart_data = None\n    if \"distance\" in streams and \"velocity_smooth\" in streams:\n        rawDist = np.array(streams[\"distance\"])\n        rawVel = np.array(streams[\"velocity_smooth\"])\n        rawAlt = np.array(streams.get(\"altitude\", []))\n        rawMoving = streams.get(\"moving\", [])\n\n        # Filter moving=false and duplicate distances\n        mask = np.ones(len(rawDist), dtype=bool)\n        for i in range(len(rawDist)):\n            if rawMoving and rawMoving[i] == False:\n                mask[i] = False\n            if i > 0 and rawDist[i] == rawDist[i - 1]:\n                mask[i] = False\n        fDist, fVel = rawDist[mask], rawVel[mask]\n        fAlt = rawAlt[mask] if len(rawAlt) else np.array([])\n\n        # Resample to 500 uniform distance points\n        N = 500\n        uDist = np.linspace(0, fDist[-1], N)\n        rVel = np.interp(uDist, fDist, fVel)\n        rAlt = np.interp(uDist, fDist, fAlt).tolist() if len(fAlt) else []\n\n        # Resample latlng for map sync\n        rawLatlng = streams.get(\"latlng\", [])\n        rLatlng = []\n        if rawLatlng:\n            fLat = np.array([ll[0] for ll in rawLatlng])[mask]\n            fLng = np.array([ll[1] for ll in rawLatlng])[mask]\n            rLat = np.interp(uDist, fDist, fLat)\n            rLng = np.interp(uDist, fDist, fLng)\n            rLatlng = [[round(float(rLat[i]), 6), round(float(rLng[i]), 6)] for i in range(N)]\n\n        # Convert to pace, apply Savitzky-Golay\n        rPace = np.where(rVel > 0.3, 1000.0 / rVel, 700.0)\n        rPace = np.minimum(rPace, 700.0)\n        smoothPace = savgol_filter(rPace, window_length=21, polyorder=3)\n        smoothPace = np.clip(smoothPace, 150, 700)\n\n        chart_data = {\n            \"dist\": (uDist / 1000).tolist(),\n            \"pace\": smoothPace.tolist(),\n            \"alt\": rAlt,\n            \"latlng\": rLatlng,\n        }\n\n    # Raw latlng for segment highlighting\n    raw_latlng = streams.get(\"latlng\", [])\n    raw_dist = streams.get(\"distance\", [])\n\n    return render_template(\"activity.html\", a=activity, splits=splits,\n                           best_efforts=best_efforts, laps=laps, streams=streams,\n                           segments=segments, chart_data=chart_data,\n                           raw_latlng=raw_latlng, raw_dist=raw_dist,\n                           format_pace=format_pace, format_duration=format_duration)\n\n\n# --- Workout routes ---\n\n@app.route(\"/workout/<int:workout_id>\")\ndef workout_detail(workout_id):\n    conn = get_db()\n    workout = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM workouts WHERE id = ?\", (workout_id,)).fetchone()\n    if not workout:\n        conn.close()\n        return \"Workout not found\", 404\n\n    exercises = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT we.*, e.name as exercise_name\n        FROM workout_exercises we\n        JOIN exercises e ON we.exercise_id = e.id\n        WHERE we.workout_id = ?\n        ORDER BY we.position\n    \"\"\", (workout_id,)).fetchall()\n\n    exercise_list = []\n    for ex in exercises:\n        ex_dict = dict(ex)\n        sets = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM sets WHERE workout_exercise_id = ? ORDER BY set_number\n        \"\"\", (ex[\"id\"],)).fetchall()\n        ex_dict[\"sets\"] = {s[\"set_number\"]: dict(s) for s in sets}\n        exercise_list.append(ex_dict)\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # Group exercises by superset\n    exercise_groups = []\n    current_group = None\n    for ex in exercise_list:\n        sg = ex.get(\"superset_group\")\n        if sg is not None:\n            if current_group and current_group[\"superset_group\"] == sg:\n                current_group[\"exercises\"].append(ex)\n            else:\n                current_group = {\"is_superset\": True, \"superset_group\": sg, \"exercises\": [ex]}\n                exercise_groups.append(current_group)\n        else:\n            current_group = None\n            exercise_groups.append({\"is_superset\": False, \"exercises\": [ex]})\n\n    return render_template(\"workout.html\", workout=dict(workout),\n                           exercise_groups=exercise_groups,\n                           format_target=format_target)\n\n\n@app.route(\"/workout/<int:workout_id>/log\", methods=[\"POST\"])\ndef workout_log(workout_id):\n    data = request.get_json()\n    sets = data.get(\"sets\", [])\n    conn = get_db()\n    count = 0\n    for s in sets:\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sets (workout_exercise_id, set_number, reps, duration_seconds, weight, rpe, notes)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n        \"\"\", (\n            s[\"workout_exercise_id\"], s[\"set_number\"],\n            s.get(\"reps\"), s.get(\"duration_seconds\"),\n            s.get(\"weight\"), s.get(\"rpe\"), s.get(\"notes\"),\n        ))\n        count += 1\n    # Mark as in_progress if still planned\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE workouts SET status = 'in_progress' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'planned'\", (workout_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return jsonify({\"ok\": True, \"count\": count})\n\n\n@app.route(\"/workout/<int:workout_id>/exercise\", methods=[\"POST\"])\ndef workout_add_exercise(workout_id):\n    data = request.get_json()\n    conn = get_db()\n\n    exercise_name = data.get(\"exercise_name\", \"\").strip()\n    if not exercise_name:\n        return jsonify({\"error\": \"Exercise name required\"}), 400\n\n    # Get or create exercise\n    normalized = exercise_name.lower().strip().rstrip(\"s\").replace(\" \", \"-\")\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT id FROM exercises WHERE LOWER(name) = ?\", (normalized,)).fetchone()\n    if row:\n        exercise_id = row[\"id\"]\n    else:\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO exercises (name, type) VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                     (normalized, \"hold\" if data.get(\"target_duration_seconds\") else \"strength\"))\n        exercise_id = conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n\n    # Auto position\n    pos_row = conn.execute(\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position), 0) + 1 as pos FROM workout_exercises WHERE workout_id = ?\",\n                           (workout_id,)).fetchone()\n\n    conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO workout_exercises (workout_id, exercise_id, position, variation, target_sets, target_reps, target_duration_seconds, notes)\n        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n    \"\"\", (workout_id, exercise_id, pos_row[\"pos\"],\n          data.get(\"variation\", \"standard\"),\n          data.get(\"target_sets\"), data.get(\"target_reps\"),\n          data.get(\"target_duration_seconds\"), data.get(\"notes\")))\n    we_id = conn.execute(\"SELECT last_insert_rowid()\").fetchone()[0]\n    conn.commit()\n\n    result = {\n        \"id\": we_id, \"exercise_name\": normalized,\n        \"variation\": data.get(\"variation\", \"standard\"),\n        \"target_sets\": data.get(\"target_sets\"),\n        \"target_reps\": data.get(\"target_reps\"),\n        \"target_duration_seconds\": data.get(\"target_duration_seconds\"),\n        \"notes\": data.get(\"notes\"), \"position\": pos_row[\"pos\"],\n    }\n    conn.close()\n    return jsonify(result)\n\n\n@app.route(\"/workout/<int:workout_id>/exercise/<int:exercise_id>\", methods=[\"DELETE\"])\ndef workout_remove_exercise(workout_id, exercise_id):\n    conn = get_db()\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM sets WHERE workout_exercise_id = ?\", (exercise_id,))\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM workout_exercises WHERE id = ? AND workout_id = ?\", (exercise_id, workout_id))\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return jsonify({\"ok\": True})\n\n\n@app.route(\"/workout/<int:workout_id>/status\", methods=[\"POST\"])\ndef workout_status(workout_id):\n    data = request.get_json()\n    status = data.get(\"status\", \"completed\")\n    conn = get_db()\n    if status == \"completed\":\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE workouts SET status = ?, completed_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, workout_id))\n    else:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE workouts SET status = ? WHERE id = ?\", (status, workout_id))\n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    return jsonify({\"ok\": True})\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    app.run(host=\"127.0.0.1\", port=5050)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/fitness_web/templates/workout.html",
  "content": "{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block title %}{{ workout.title }} — {{ workout.date }}{% endblock %}\n\n{% block content %}\n<div class=\"workout-header\">\n    <h1>{{ workout.title }}</h1>\n    <div class=\"meta\">{{ workout.date }} <span class=\"badge\" id=\"statusBadge\">{{ workout.status }}</span></div>\n    {% if workout.notes %}<p class=\"workout-notes\">{{ workout.notes }}</p>{% endif %}\n</div>\n\n{% for group in exercise_groups %}\n{% if group.is_superset %}\n<div class=\"superset-block\">\n    <div class=\"superset-label\">Superset</div>\n{% endif %}\n\n{% for ex in group.exercises %}\n<div class=\"exercise-card\" data-exercise-id=\"{{ ex.id }}\">\n    <div class=\"exercise-header\">\n        <div class=\"exercise-info\">\n            <span class=\"exercise-name\">{{ ex.exercise_name }}</span>\n            {% if ex.variation and ex.variation != 'standard' %}\n            <span class=\"variation-badge\">{{ ex.variation }}</span>\n            {% endif %}\n        </div>\n        <span class=\"exercise-target\">{{ format_target(ex) }}</span>\n        <button class=\"remove-exercise-btn\" title=\"Remove\">&times;</button>\n    </div>\n    {% if ex.notes %}<div class=\"exercise-note\">{{ ex.notes }}</div>{% endif %}\n\n    <div class=\"sets-table\">\n        <div class=\"sets-header\">\n            <span class=\"set-col\">Set</span>\n            {% if ex.target_duration_seconds %}\n            <span class=\"input-col\">Seconds</span>\n            {% else %}\n            <span class=\"input-col\">Reps</span>\n            {% endif %}\n            {% if ex.target_weight %}<span class=\"input-col\">kg</span>{% endif %}\n            <span class=\"input-col-sm\">RPE</span>\n        </div>\n        {% for s in range(1, (ex.target_sets or 3) + 1) %}\n        <div class=\"set-row\" data-set-number=\"{{ s }}\">\n            <span class=\"set-col set-number\">{{ s }}</span>\n            {% if ex.target_duration_seconds %}\n            <input type=\"number\" class=\"dur-input\" inputmode=\"numeric\" pattern=\"[0-9]*\"\n                   placeholder=\"{{ ex.target_duration_seconds }}\"\n                   value=\"{{ ex.sets[s].duration_seconds if s in ex.sets and ex.sets[s].duration_seconds else '' }}\">\n            {% else %}\n            <input type=\"number\" class=\"reps-input\" inputmode=\"numeric\" pattern=\"[0-9]*\"\n                   placeholder=\"{{ ex.target_reps or '' }}\"\n                   value=\"{{ ex.sets[s].reps if s in ex.sets and ex.sets[s].reps else '' }}\">\n            {% endif %}\n            {% if ex.target_weight %}\n            <input type=\"number\" class=\"weight-input\" inputmode=\"decimal\" step=\"0.5\"\n                   placeholder=\"{{ ex.target_weight }}\"\n                   value=\"{{ ex.sets[s].weight if s in ex.sets and ex.sets[s].weight else '' }}\">\n            {% endif %}\n            <input type=\"number\" class=\"rpe-input\" inputmode=\"decimal\" step=\"0.5\" min=\"1\" max=\"10\"\n                   placeholder=\"{{ ex.target_rpe or '' }}\"\n                   value=\"{{ ex.sets[s].rpe if s in ex.sets and ex.sets[s].rpe else '' }}\">\n        </div>\n        {% endfor %}\n    </div>\n\n    <textarea class=\"exercise-comment\" placeholder=\"Notes...\"></textarea>\n</div>\n{% endfor %}\n\n{% if group.is_superset %}\n</div>\n{% endif %}\n{% endfor %}\n\n<button class=\"add-exercise-btn\" id=\"addExerciseBtn\">+ Add Exercise</button>\n\n<div class=\"modal\" id=\"addExerciseModal\" style=\"display:none\">\n    <div class=\"modal-content\">\n        <h3>Add Exercise</h3>\n        <input type=\"text\" id=\"newExerciseName\" placeholder=\"Exercise name\">\n        <select id=\"newExerciseVariation\">\n            <option value=\"standard\">Standard</option>\n            <option value=\"negative\">Negative</option>\n            <option value=\"assisted\">Assisted</option>\n            <option value=\"band-assisted\">Band-assisted</option>\n            <option value=\"weighted\">Weighted</option>\n            <option value=\"half\">Half rep</option>\n            <option value=\"eccentric\">Eccentric</option>\n            <option value=\"wide\">Wide</option>\n            <option value=\"narrow\">Narrow</option>\n            <option value=\"diamond\">Diamond</option>\n            <option value=\"archer\">Archer</option>\n        </select>\n        <div class=\"modal-row\">\n            <input type=\"number\" id=\"newExerciseSets\" placeholder=\"Sets\" value=\"3\" inputmode=\"numeric\">\n            <input type=\"number\" id=\"newExerciseReps\" placeholder=\"Reps\" inputmode=\"numeric\">\n            <input type=\"number\" id=\"newExerciseDuration\" placeholder=\"Seconds\" inputmode=\"numeric\">\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"modal-actions\">\n            <button id=\"cancelAddExercise\" class=\"btn-secondary\">Cancel</button>\n            <button id=\"confirmAddExercise\" class=\"btn-primary\">Add</button>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"workout-actions\">\n    <button class=\"save-btn\" id=\"saveBtn\">Save Progress</button>\n    <button class=\"finish-btn\" id=\"finishBtn\">Finish Workout</button>\n</div>\n<div class=\"save-status\" id=\"saveStatus\"></div>\n{% endblock %}\n\n{% block scripts %}\n<script>\nconst WORKOUT_ID = {{ workout.id }};\nlet saveTimer = null;\n\nfunction collectSets() {\n    const sets = [];\n    document.querySelectorAll('.exercise-card').forEach(card => {\n        const exerciseId = parseInt(card.dataset.exerciseId);\n        card.querySelectorAll('.set-row').forEach(row => {\n            const setNumber = parseInt(row.dataset.setNumber);\n            const reps = row.querySelector('.reps-input')?.value || null;\n            const dur = row.querySelector('.dur-input')?.value || null;\n            const weight = row.querySelector('.weight-input')?.value || null;\n            const rpe = row.querySelector('.rpe-input')?.value || null;\n            if (reps || dur || weight || rpe) {\n                sets.push({\n                    workout_exercise_id: exerciseId,\n                    set_number: setNumber,\n                    reps: reps ? parseInt(reps) : null,\n                    duration_seconds: dur ? parseInt(dur) : null,\n                    weight: weight ? parseFloat(weight) : null,\n                    rpe: rpe ? parseFloat(rpe) : null,\n                });\n            }\n        });\n    });\n    return sets;\n}\n\nfunction save() {\n    const sets = collectSets();\n    const status = document.getElementById('saveStatus');\n    status.textContent = 'Saving...';\n    fetch(`/workout/${WORKOUT_ID}/log`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},\n        body: JSON.stringify({sets}),\n    })\n    .then(r => r.json())\n    .then(d => {\n        status.textContent = d.ok ? 'Saved' : 'Error saving';\n        if (d.ok) {\n            const badge = document.getElementById('statusBadge');\n            if (badge.textContent === 'planned') badge.textContent = 'in_progress';\n        }\n    })\n    .catch(() => { status.textContent = 'Failed to save'; });\n}\n\nfunction scheduleSave() {\n    clearTimeout(saveTimer);\n    document.getElementById('saveStatus').textContent = 'Unsaved changes...';\n    saveTimer = setTimeout(save, 800);\n}\n\n// Auto-save on input\ndocument.querySelectorAll('.exercise-card input, .exercise-card textarea').forEach(el => {\n    el.addEventListener('input', scheduleSave);\n});\n\n// Manual save\ndocument.getElementById('saveBtn').addEventListener('click', save);\n\n// Finish workout\ndocument.getElementById('finishBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {\n    save();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n        fetch(`/workout/${WORKOUT_ID}/status`, {\n            method: 'POST',\n            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},\n            body: JSON.stringify({status: 'completed'}),\n        })\n        .then(r => r.json())\n        .then(() => {\n            document.getElementById('statusBadge').textContent = 'completed';\n            document.getElementById('finishBtn').disabled = true;\n            document.getElementById('finishBtn').textContent = 'Done';\n            document.getElementById('saveStatus').textContent = 'Workout complete';\n        });\n    }, 1000);\n});\n\n// Remove exercise\ndocument.querySelectorAll('.remove-exercise-btn').forEach(btn => {\n    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {\n        const card = this.closest('.exercise-card');\n        const eid = card.dataset.exerciseId;\n        if (confirm('Remove this exercise?')) {\n            fetch(`/workout/${WORKOUT_ID}/exercise/${eid}`, {method: 'DELETE'})\n            .then(r => r.json())\n            .then(() => card.remove());\n        }\n    });\n});\n\n// Add exercise modal\ndocument.getElementById('addExerciseBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {\n    document.getElementById('addExerciseModal').style.display = 'flex';\n});\ndocument.getElementById('cancelAddExercise').addEventListener('click', () => {\n    document.getElementById('addExerciseModal').style.display = 'none';\n});\ndocument.getElementById('confirmAddExercise').addEventListener('click', () => {\n    const name = document.getElementById('newExerciseName').value.trim();\n    if (!name) return;\n    const data = {\n        exercise_name: name,\n        variation: document.getElementById('newExerciseVariation').value,\n        target_sets: parseInt(document.getElementById('newExerciseSets').value) || 3,\n        target_reps: parseInt(document.getElementById('newExerciseReps').value) || null,\n        target_duration_seconds: parseInt(document.getElementById('newExerciseDuration').value) || null,\n    };\n    fetch(`/workout/${WORKOUT_ID}/exercise`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},\n        body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    })\n    .then(r => r.json())\n    .then(ex => {\n        document.getElementById('addExerciseModal').style.display = 'none';\n        // Reload to show new exercise\n        window.location.reload();\n    });\n});\n</script>\n{% endblock %}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/web/argus/argus/fitness_web/templates/index.html",
  "content": "{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block title %}Fitness{% endblock %}\n\n{% block content %}\n{% if workouts %}\n<h1>Workouts</h1>\n<table>\n    <thead>\n        <tr>\n            <th>Date</th>\n            <th>Workout</th>\n            <th>Exercises</th>\n            <th>Status</th>\n        </tr>\n    </thead>\n    <tbody>\n        {% for w in workouts %}\n        <tr onclick=\"window.location='/workout/{{ w.id }}'\" class=\"clickable\">\n            <td>{{ w.date }}</td>\n            <td>{{ w.title or 'Untitled' }}</td>\n            <td>{{ w.exercise_count }}</td>\n            <td><span class=\"badge\">{{ w.status }}</span></td>\n        </tr>\n        {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n</table>\n{% endif %}\n\n<h1>Activities</h1>\n\n{% if activities %}\n<table>\n    <thead>\n        <tr>\n            <th>Date</th>\n            <th>Name</th>\n            <th>Type</th>\n            <th>Distance</th>\n            <th>Time</th>\n            <th>Pace</th>\n            <th>Elevation</th>\n            <th>HR</th>\n            <th>Calories</th>\n        </tr>\n    </thead>\n    <tbody>\n        {% for a in activities %}\n        <tr onclick=\"window.location='/activity/{{ a.id }}'\" class=\"clickable\">\n            <td>{{ a.date }}</td>\n            <td>{{ a.name }}</td>\n            <td><span class=\"badge\">{{ a.type }}</span></td>\n            <td>{{ \"%.2f\"|format(a.distance / 1000) }} km</td>\n            <td>{{ format_duration(a.moving_time) }}</td>\n            <td>{{ format_pace(a.average_speed) }}</td>\n            <td>{{ \"%.0f\"|format(a.total_elevation_gain or 0) }}m</td>\n            <td>{{ \"%.0f\"|format(a.average_heartrate) if a.average_heartrate else \"-\" }}</td>\n            <td>{{ \"%.0f\"|format(a.calories) if a.calories else \"-\" }}</td>\n        </tr>\n        {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n</table>\n{% else %}\n<p>No activities yet. Sync from Strava to get started.</p>\n{% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n"
}

  "new_string": "async def handle_workout(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    \"\"\"Generate a workout plan via chat and send the link.\"\"\"\n    if not is_authorized(update):\n        return\n    if not _is_admin(update):\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"That command is admin-only.\")\n        return\n\n    user_id = update.effective_user.id\n    user_msg = update.message.text.replace(\"/workout\", \"\").strip()\n    if not user_msg:\n        user_msg = \"I want to do a workout. Ask me what equipment I have available and what I want to focus on, then create a plan.\"\n\n    log.info(f\"Workout command from {update.effective_user.username}: {user_msg[:100]}\")\n\n    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()\n    done = asyncio.Event()\n\n    async def keep_typing():\n        while not done.is_set():\n            try:\n                await update.message.chat.send_action(\"typing\")\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            try:\n                await asyncio.wait_for(done.wait(), timeout=4)\n                break\n            except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n                pass\n\n    typing_task = asyncio.create_task(keep_typing())\n\n    def on_status(text: str):\n        try:\n            future = asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(\n                _send_and_log(update, text), loop)\n            future.result(timeout=10)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    try:\n        reply = await loop.run_in_executor(\n            None, lambda: chat(user_msg, user_id=user_id, on_status=on_status))\n    except Exception as e:\n        log.error(f\"Error: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n        reply = f\"Something went wrong: {e}\"\n    finally:\n        done.set()\n        await typing_task\n\n    safe = _html_safe(reply)\n    for i in range(0, len(safe), 4096):\n        await update.message.reply_text(safe[i : i + 4096], parse_mode=\"HTML\")\n\n\nasync def post_init(application):\n    \"\"\"Register bot commands so they show up in Telegram's / menu.\"\"\"\n    await application.bot.set_my_commands([\n        (\"workout\", \"Generate a workout plan\"),\n        (\"fresh\", \"Clear conversation history and start fresh\"),"
}

54	
55	**NEVER invent details.** No made-up numbers, descriptions, deadlines, or context that Sumeet didn't provide. If he says "choose favourite photos", the task is exactly that — don't add "select 10-15" or a deadline he didn't mention. Only include what he actually said.
56	
57	**Project routing** (use these IDs):
58	- **Personal** (id=3): life stuff, errands, fitness, appointments, personal to-dos
59	- **Argus** (id=2): anything about extending Argus or its AI functionality
60	- **Research** (id=4): research requests, "look into X", "investigate Y"
61	- **Project Pyari** (id=5): anything related to Project Pyari. SHARED project — always use "Sumeet" not "my" or "me" in task titles
62	- **Inbox** (id=1): only if it doesn't fit the above
63	
64	**update_task / complete_task**: Change or finish ONE task.
65	
66	**search_vault / read_file**: Find notes or files. When Sumeet asks about a person, place, or anything that might be in the vault, ALWAYS use search_vault or read_file to check before saying you don't know. People files are in wiki/People/{Name}.md. Try the obvious path first, fall back to search_vault if unsure.
67	
68	**do_work**: Complex multi-step operations only. Epics with subtasks, bulk ops, health checks, audits. If it needs 3+ tool calls, use do_work. ALWAYS use do_work for epics — never try to create epics with subtasks directly.
69	
70	## LINKS
71	
72	When a tool result contains a URL (like https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39), include it naturally in your response. Don't write "Link:" — just weave the URL into the text. For example: "Done, created the epic https://task.argbox.net/tasks/39 with 3 subtasks." The URL will automatically become clickable.
73
